Tests: `gather.test.ts`, `editor/ui/localisation/localisationModels.test.ts`, `ReportView.stories.tsx`, `engine/tests/uiLocalisation.test.ts` (`:lang()` through a view). **0423 wave F (2026-11-26): T7's markup tools rebuilt for Svelte UI.** `editor/uiEdit/svelteStrings.ts` reads, from `parse()` spans (`svelteText.ts` now keeps an element's text and expression tags in order, `t("key")`/`tHtml("key")` calls with literal keys, and the instance script's imports), an element's localisable text: its text content and the literal attributes that show text (`placeholder`, `title`, `aria-label`, `alt`, an option's `label`, a button-like input's `value`), each keyed (one `{t("key")}`), literal, or dynamic (with the reason); `translate="no"` on it or an ancestor leaves it out. **Report:** the unkeyed-text list again (literal text with letters, and text mixed with expressions or elements, marked so), Dismiss kept in `.myco/localisation-ignore.json` (`unkeyedId`: file, `#id` or `<tag>`, field, text), rows opening the Code panel at the element's line (keys defined nowhere too); **Check text fit** (`textFit.ts`, `fitCheck.ts`'s logic) lays out every element the open scene's views render whose whole text is one keyed message, in each shipped language and `qps-ploc`, against its laid-out content box from the scene view (`uiViewStrings.ts` `fitTargets`; padding and border taken off; fixed only in a direction whose `width`/`height` isn't `auto`), measured with the engine's canvas measuring; keyed text no view renders, or with no fixed width, is skipped and counted. **Inspector** (`UIViewLocalise.tsx`, `LocaliseControl.tsx` again, `localiseKeys.ts` = `localiseModel.ts` for components): for each localisable part of a static element, pick a key (Localise) or Extract to string table (suggested key from the component's name and the element's id, e.g. `menu.title`; the component's own table by name, else the first), written as `{t("key")}` (or `name={t("key")}`) with `import { t } from "myco"` added to the script (into a `myco` import, after the last import, or a new `<script>`; metered: osc 0.67×, noise 0.76×, synth 1.43×, blip 2.38× (over 0360's 1.5× tripwire), silence 0.64× their sample twins; mixer 538,902 B (tripwire 576 KB). **G3:** generator entries and sequence generator clips play (PLAY of the reserved id + GEN, `gen:` curves as AUTOMATE target 5, per-trigger seed/vary/silence draws from the World generator in pick order so takes replay them, key-off on release and by `length`, the loop flag for `untilStopped`, hot reload as a partial GEN), `Audio.play` of `*.generator.json`, silence forced virtual, cap weights from the measured ratios (`selection.ts` `GENERATOR_VOICE_COST`), REPLAY re-sends GEN, debug rows and captures name generators; the core's memory answer covers GenEntries. **G2b (D67):** blip optimised to 1.47× a sample voice (1.02–1.36× per preset), `quality` (standard PolyBLEP; high 16-tap minBLEP: −80/−76/−70 dB worst alias at 0.25/1.2/3.5 kHz). **G4:** Studio Generator editor in the Playlists screen (catalogue rows, presets, Randomise/Mutate/seed/locks, kind switch, preview through the offline render, audition with Space and keys, library, Samples provenance, Profiler kinds). **G5:** `myco_offline_generate` (offline ABI 1.2, bit-identical to the core), `bakeGenerator` (WAV + sidecar provenance, variations, Queue, Replace uses), Studio Bake and Rebake, the Babylon fallback plays cached renders, exports ship the offline module only for generator projects. **G6:** `docs/wiki/guide/sound-generators.md`. **Missing:** G6 e2e, quantised `gen:` curves, mid-envelope resume after a rebuild, `gen:` voice curves in the curve editor, auditioning embedded generators below a Clip's root. Builds in G1–G6 (format, core and wire ABI 5.1, runtime, offline bake and fallback, Studio Generator editor, docs and e2e). The per-voice cost table is an estimate until G2's metered scenes. |
| [0361](../decisions/0361-the-audio-studio-adapts-to-screen-size-touch-and-assistive-tech-with-one-icon-set.md) Studio screen size, touch, assistive tech, icons | Done | `Icon` (stroke set incl. Spatial object kinds), `TransportButton`; width-driven header, player bar and status bar; `DockHost.foldBelow` + `narrowFold.ts` (Studio 900px) and the one-panel phone view in `ScreenDock`; `pointer: coarse` sizes, `longPress.ts`, reduced motion, focus rings; every screen (Samples, Playlists, Dialogue, Mixer, Spatial, Take, Profiler, Packages) and the shared components use icons, wrap or scroll at 360px, and name icon-only controls; zoom buttons on the Samples timeline, Sequence and CausalChain (Waveform has an opt-in `zoomButtons`). **Missing:** pinch-zoom on timelines; keyboard re-route of buses, nudging Spatial objects and Samples timeline markers by keyboard; checked items in the shared ContextMenu; the Sequence track header width is fixed (168px) on narrow docks; touch drag of library samples and tree rows (Move to… and Enter are the touch routes). |
| [0362](../decisions/0362-the-ui-fidelity-pass-adds-playback-pause-clip-envelopes-reverse-second-input-curves-occlusion-curves-and-studio-format-fields.md) UI pass engine and format additions | Done | §1 `AudioPlaylistHandle.pause/resume/paused` (holds at the earliest stampable time; stamped records wait on the core); §2 clip Amp envelope per voice in the core (AMP_ENVELOPE, ABI 6.1, `audio_dsp::adsr` shared with the offline render, `hold` stage); §3 `reverse: true` clips play backwards (loops backwards; reversed sync loops drive the playhead; async reversed clips report markers); §4 marker and sample reserved-range `colour`/`note`, folder colours in `audio.json` `studio.folderColours`; §5 `by` on playlist voice curves, sequence automation and Mixer bus/effect automation (`engine/audio/curveBy.ts`); §6 occlusion curves (`settings.occlusion.curves`, op `setOcclusionCurves`); §7 `snapshotFolders`, `Bus.colour`, parameter `type`, ops `contains`/`starts`, test-scene emitter `output`; §8 loudness defaults. The UI pass itself: decision log D71–D73, `docs/design/audio-studio/ui-pass/`. **Missing:** resume heard after the main-thread promote (≤1 frame + 5 ms); reversed ADPCM silent; Web Audio fallback plays reversed clips forward without envelopes; takes don't record pause/resume.

## Known gaps worth fixing next

- Transform columns for systems (0064's open question): moving transforms through the facade is the slowest measured path (13 ms for 50k nodes; see the 0012 row).
- Production bundle size: the old figure (~4 MiB, mostly Rapier's inlined WASM) predates the switch to Babylon.js; re-measure the `npm run build` output (player and editor) before relying on it.
- The quality tier's `anisotropy` field is never applied on Babylon (0050), and a physical shader graph's layer values (clear coat roughness, transmission, IOR, iridescence) aren't fed to the material (0052).
- Deferred items in the newest records: the raw shader target and a code editor with inline errors (0106), static batching (0108), WebGPU checks for render textures and sky (0109, 0112).
- Stale code comments left by the three.js and uikit removal (0076, 0081): `vite.player.config.ts`, `render/components.ts` (`Text`), and `RenderBackend` mentions in `RenderSurface.ts`, `navGizmo.ts`, `editorOverlays.ts`.
| [0370](../decisions/0370-animation-evaluates-on-an-engine-owned-rust-wasm-simd-core.md) Animation core in Rust/wasm | Partial | **Amended by [0378](../decisions/0378-the-animation-builds-api-additions-and-differences-amend-0370-to-0376.md)** (the build's added names and its differences from this record's text). **Phase B:** skinning (T16): `mycoSkin` in KSL (`render/skinning/`, 4 or 8 influences, morphs first, weights re-normalised) on the GPU kernel executor; WebGPU compute into a Babylon vertex storage buffer, WebGL2 transform feedback into a vertex buffer of the same layout (stride 48), a CPU fallback; one palette upload per skeleton and pose version, dispatch only on change; bounds from the used joints' transformed rest boxes; shadows, depth and previews reuse the skinned buffer (`render/babylon/skinnedMeshes.ts`, `plugins/animation/runtime/features/skinning/`); tests `engine/tests/skinKernel.test.ts`, `skinRuns.test.ts`, `babylonSkinning.test.ts`, `plugins/animation/skinning.test.ts`. Not verified on a GPU (the coordinator's capture spec, T81). Missing: 2D skinned sprites (T21), editor picking of GPU-skinned meshes, script Material instances on skinned meshes, sparse morph targets. The host (T13t, `plugins/animation/runtime/core/`): a lazy loader (one instance per page; Node and the dedicated server read the wasm from disk), simd128 detection (warns once, reports unsupported), INIT honouring MEMORY_NEEDED (defaults 512 instances, 256 bones, 4 MiB arena), the `AnimationCore` host with a generic wire codec and per-World command queues, the `myco.animation.core` World service (a namespace per World, assets reference-counted across Worlds, idle assets released when an Engine is disposed, i.e. between editor play sessions), and `frameFor`/`animation.Evaluate` evaluating through it; tests `runtime/core/*.test.ts`. Missing: project-settings limits. Also: the shared GPU kernel executor `render/gpuKernels/` (T15): `GpuKernel` (WGSL compute, cached bind groups, readback), `GlKernel` (GLSL pass into RGBA32F targets), layout helpers, and item kernels (one KSL body as WGSL compute, GLSL fragment and JavaScript; `GpuItemKernel` with a vertex-bindable output, `GlItemKernel`, `JsItemKernel`); the particle runners run on it, with generated shaders pinned byte for byte by `engine/tests/particleKernelShaderText.test.ts` (snapshots from before the move). **Phase R:** **Phase R** (plan `docs/design/animation/plan.md`). Built (T02): `native/anim/` workspace (Rust 1.99.1, `+simd128` in `.cargo/config.toml`), the 16 crates as stubs with their dependency edges, the wire mechanism (`anim-wire/src/record.rs`, ten families, `gen_wire` writing `src/plugins/animation/runtime/wasm/wire/<family>.ts` and vectors), `init`/memory-needed and `source_hash` exports, `npm run build:anim` with a 30 KB size gate (tripwire), the source-hash staleness test and per-family round-trip tests (`src/plugins/animation/runtime/wasm/*.test.ts`, `anim-wire/tests/families.rs`). `anim-math` (T10): SoA vector, quaternion and transform maths on `simd128` with a bit-identical portable fallback, deterministic polynomial trig (no libm), row-major 3×4 bone matrices, pose transposes with identity padding, local→model over `u16` parents (parents first), N-bit unorm quantisation; `anim-offline::math` is the f64 reference; its tests also run on wasm32 in Node (`crates/anim-math/wasm-test-runner.mjs`). `anim-format` (T03r): `myco.anim@1` (raw samples, ACL-style compressed descriptors, 32-byte key lists, root curve), `myco.skel@1`, `myco.animgraph@1` (parameters, nodes, state machines with priority transitions, structured conditions, slots, links, layers, choosers), `myco.posedb@1`, with validating zero-copy readers, self-checking writers and round-trip and corruption tests. `anim-pose` (T12): blend, lerp, masked blend, additive, mirror, model space and 3×4 skinning matrices, root-motion delta and bake-out, sync phase, inertialization (Bollo quintic with a no-overshoot clamp), tested against `anim-offline::pose` and on wasm32. `anim-compress` and `anim-sample` (T11): uniform resampling, default and constant channels, clip and 16-frame segment range reduction, per-segment bit rates searched against ACL's object-space error (bones) and absolute error (properties), root curve, size and error report; bone pose and property sampling from compressed and raw forms with a segment cache and no allocation; tested against `anim-offline::sample` natively and on wasm32. Track kinds (T20r): a per-clip binding table (bit-identical, about 35% faster) and windowed sampling in `anim-sample` (`window.rs`): discrete, activation, morph and value columns; events gated by weight; notify states begin/tick/end; markers and sync phase; subclip states; control and audio records; the `tracks` wire family and record writer (`anim-wasm/src/tracks.rs`). Boundary and minimal graph (T13r): `anim-wasm` with an asset arena (one aligned block per asset, handles index+generation), the instance table with snapshot/restore, `write_inputs`/`evaluate(world, pass, dt)` with update-rate LOD and culling, root, matrix, change and track views; the `anim-graph` interpreter (`Program`, per-node state in one blob, pulled phases) with clip player, blend2, crossfade, layer and inertialize nodes; code-first `play` builds an implicit graph (4 layers × two players and a crossfade); a Node test runs the committed wasm against `anim-offline`. Rig solvers (T40, solver half): `anim-rig` with two-bone (analytic, soft IK, stretch, pole), CCD and FABRIK with swing-twist limits, look-at and aim, multi-effector full-body FABRIK with sub-base fitting and a centre-of-mass goal, grounder (ray requests, foot targets, pelvis offset, half-life smoothing), world-space Verlet spring bones with colliders, copy/limit/damp constraints, distance/stride/orientation/motion warping; tested against `anim-offline::rig` and on wasm (golden hash across targets). Tweens and timelines (T22r): the core tween table (31 Penner easings by id, deterministic; loops, yoyo, relative, quaternion, time modes, kill and destroyed records) and timeline instances (a clip used as a timeline, nested subclips to 3 levels, crossfades, sequences built from tween specs, wrap modes, silent seeks). **Crates since [0424](../decisions/0424-tweening-is-a-shared-rust-crate-linked-by-the-animation-core-and-a-small-tween-module.md):** the tween table, its value function, the Penner easings and the sequence program are `myco-tween` (`native/common/tween`), the record mechanism (`wire_record!`, record and vector types) is `myco-wire` (`native/common/wire`), and the curve key parser is `myco-curve`'s behind its `alloc` feature; `anim-wasm` links `myco-tween` (its `tweens.rs` is the table's share of `init` and `impl Curves for Arena`; `timeline.rs` plays `myco_tween::seq`), `anim-wire` re-exports `myco-wire` and the `tween` family (its generator unchanged), `anim-sample` re-exports the parser, and `exp2` moved to `anim_math::trig::exp2`. The core's source hash covers all of `native/common/`. Retargeting (T41, solver half): the Humanoid skeleton profile (Godot's 56 roles plus 8 twist roles, joint ranges, mirror pairs, chains), a validated profile boundary encoding, `guess_bone_map` (Mixamo, Unreal mannequin, Rigify, VRM/VRoid and 3ds Max Biped fully mapped in tests), chain retargeting with rest-pose compensation and alignment, leg-ratio root scaling, IK goals with two-bone pinning, offline bake (raw, optional compression), and the per-bone mirror flip table in `anim-pose`. Full graph (T31): state machines with a blend stack, priority and any-state transitions, `when`/`how` with bridges, interrupt rules, sub-machines, requests, `travel` and `jump`; 1D and 2D blend spaces (deterministic Delaunay, hull projection, glide, marker sync, speed scaling); pose snapshot, reference and bind pose; random selector and chooser; blend by enum and bool, additive, time scale, mirror; slots with sections and groups; linked graphs; sync groups and notify gate; the typed blackboard; inertialization routed to the requester's ancestor; the debug report with transition blockers; snapshot/restore exact through all of it; a Node test runs a locomotion graph through the wasm. Motion matching (T42, solver half): the database bake (tagged ranges, default 27-dim schema, per-dimension normalisation, KD-tree, deterministic bytes), the database load, SIMD brute-force and exact KD search returning identical results, tag filters, spring trajectory prediction, the query builder and the matching controller (search interval, forced search, continuation bias, minimum switch time). Third-party nodes, Rust side (T45): ABI v1 and the SDK in `anim-plugin-sdk` (no_std, compile-time descriptors, batch evaluation with core-owned state), validation and the fuel pass in `anim-meter` (no_std, deterministic, re-metering refused), and `native/anim/examples/plugin-sample` tested end to end in wasmi. Rig node family (T40, graph half): 16 rig node kinds (two-bone, CCD, FABRIK and full-body IK, look-at, aim, grounder, spring chain, copy, rotation and position limits, damp, stride, orientation and motion warp, distance match) with documented word layouts, weights from parameters or curves, rig LOD with reset on re-enable, joint limits and Humanoid roles from the skeleton, solver state in the instance blob (snapshot-exact), and the `rig` wire family (IK target slots, ray requests and hits, `WARP_TARGET`, `RIG_RESET`). Retarget node and physics (T41 node half, T44r): the `RETARGET` node runs a nested source graph on the source skeleton and retargets its output (root axes, pin weights), MIRROR applies per-bone flips, setup exports (`profile_decode`, `guess_bone_map`, `retarget_map_create`, mirror table, raw bake; compressed bake behind the `editor` cargo feature); `anim-physics` (ragdoll descriptions and builder, drive targets relative to the parent body, angular velocity, strengths and pins, body→local, get-up facing), `PHYSICAL_POSE` and `PHYSICS_BLEND` nodes and the `physics` wire family (input bodies, drive output, capture). Matching node and plugin nodes (T42 node half, T45 core half): the `POSE_DATABASE` node (predictor and controller state in the blob, query from the instance's pose history, tag filters from words, parameters and chooser `MATCH_TAGS` rows, search interval × LOD, inertialized switches, root motion from the matched clip) and the `match` debug output; pose databases as arena assets; the `PLUGIN_NODE` family calling third-party modules through the `mycoAnimPluginHost.call` import (SoA↔AoS, descriptor defaults, parameter bindings, fault pass-through reported once), `plugin_register`, and a Node test with a real JS host running the metered sample plugin. **Third-party graph and rig nodes, TypeScript side (T45t):** `plugin.json` `animationNodes` (lenient: warnings, never refusal); a loader with editor-wasm metering (`meter_node_module`) and a content cache; modules metered at export; host glue with fuel (`budget × count + base`), trap re-instantiation and one warning per node instance (`PLUGIN_FAULT` records); descriptor-generated catalogue kinds with named, clamped parameters and a registry (`pluginNodeKinds()`) for the graph editor; plugin calls receive the update phase's dt. Lacks: batched calls by node level (D78), npm-plugin node modules, the graph editor listing plugin kinds. Benchmarks and performance (T82r): criterion benches (`anim-bench`) and the Node budget bench (`anim-wasm/bench_budgets.mjs`); a per-player frame cache, rig cache by ancestor chain, KD rows in leaf order with per-node bounding boxes (exact box bound, D147), per-clip segment range tables (D146), grouped SIMD frame decode straight into the cache's SoA slot (D144), masked slot decode, no per-sample re-binding, matrix skip for still poses, all bit-identical; single-threaded by owner decision (D145); the name section stripped; clippy clean. **Follow-ups (T70b):** a per-instance server eval mask (`set_eval_mask`: only the bones BoneAttachments follow, and their ancestors, get matrices on a server), an in-place raw→compiled clip swap (`replace_clip`, same handle and plays, asset epoch invalidation), match debug reports carrying the predicted and matched trajectories (match wire v2), `posedb_bake` mirrored copies (posedb sections `CLIP_FLAGS`/`MIRROR`, played mirrored at run time) and curve-filled custom features. **`myco-curve` (T70a):** `native/common/curve/` (no_std, f32 and f64 forms) used by audio-core with bit-identical output (a 1.28 M-evaluation sweep hashed the same) and by the animation core: curve assets (`load_curve`), curve easing for tweens and sequences (an `ease` above 30 is a curve handle), `curve_sample` in the runtime and editor modules; parity with `core/curve.ts` (f64 exact, f32 within 1e-6 of scale, Bézier as a 4-ulp backward error); the source hashes cover `native/common/`. **T70c:** curve tweens (one core asset per distinct curve per World, released on end and World close), runtime raw-clip curve tracks and the editor compile step all use the core evaluator, so compiled clips match the runtime bit for bit; D114's host-side easing is retired. **Benches through the shipped player (T82):** `npm run bench:anim` (`tools/anim-bench/`) exports a generated project with the shipped player and times the animation stages in headless Chromium through public probe systems, plus a size-gate table. Missing:  server-World matrix skip; the character budget (2.80 ms in Node, T82r; 4.6–6.4 ms evaluate through the player in the container, T82 — a run on the 0370 reference machine with a real GPU is needed before the threads decision); the TypeScript host and formats, the ECS, rendering and the editor (Phase B). |

Sub-path animatability is done (T43). Clip compile pipeline (T19): a separate editor wasm (`anim-editor-wasm`: compression, skeleton compile, compressed retarget bake, pose-database bake; `runtime/wasm/editor/`, 341 KB, gate 410 KB), compression settings inherited project → folder rule → sidecar with a platform override at each level (Project Settings > Animation), an editor compile worker with a content-keyed `.myco/anim/` cache and reports, runtime resolution (build file → editor compiled → raw form made in TypeScript), exports compiling referenced clips to `*.anim.bin` and model skeletons to `*.skel.bin`; tests `compile/*.test.ts`, `clipSources.node.test.ts`, `editor/compile/*.test.ts`, `export/exportAnimation.test.ts`. Live material animation (T24): animated `KHR_texture_transform` stays live on a surface's textures (`render/babylon/textureTransform.ts`; identical to the baked UVs when unchanged), per-slot material overrides `MeshRenderer.setMaterialProperty(name, value, slot)` backing `materials[slot].<prop>` tracks (each overridden slot its own clone, set in place, freed with the renderer), light and camera fields proven to sync every frame; tests `babylonLiveMaterial.test.ts`, `animatedMaterialLight.test.ts`, `babylonAnimatedCamera.test.ts`. Missing: skeleton roles in compiled skeletons, (import, the clip pipeline, sprites, tweens, key buttons and saved bone maps are built: T14, T19, T21, T22t, T50, T54). |

| [0402](../decisions/0402-ghost-creation-goes-reliably-apart-from-per-tick-state.md) Reliable ghost lifecycle | Done | **Amended by [0405](../decisions/0405-late-snapshot-parts-are-read-in-tick-order-and-acked.md)** (late parts read and acked). **Server** `net/replication/server.ts`: `lifecycleMessage` writes a connection's tick of despawns, leaves, relayouts (with the full state), structure changes and spawns (with the full state; id order, while the tick's budget lasts, at least one) and sends it as `net.ghosts` on the reliable channel, once per event (`PeerGhost.spawnSent`, `layoutSent`, `structureSent`); `lifecycleAcked` applies an ack of every message up to a tick (spawn acked, layout acked, the full state as baseline, only for the ghost's current stay, `since`); `snapshot` puts a ghost's state in a part only once `spawnAcked` and `layoutAcked` reach its layout; parts carry states only (`write`, `pack` without pending items); the string table marks a string acked as soon as it's written; `lastLifecycleBytes` beside `lastBytes`, and the Network panel's snapshot bytes are their sum. **Wire** `GHOSTS` and the ack's leading lifecycle tick (`wire.ts`); protocol 6 (`net/session.ts`); the Network panel counts `net.ghosts` as snapshots (`editor/netPlay.ts`). **Client** `net/replication/client.ts`: `readLifecycle` before snapshots each frame; a part older than the newest lifecycle message applied is dropped; acks carry the lifecycle tick. **Tests** `src/engine/tests/replicationParts.test.ts` ("spawns go reliably, apart from states": a test transport whose stream, reliable messages and parts over a datagram, lags its datagrams by 150 ms; a ghost of 1,200 bytes spawns with its values and later small changes arrive (fails before: never spawns); waves of spawns and destroys under 30% loss and reordering with no read errors and every ghost converging; a big ghost destroyed while its spawn is in flight leaves nothing; a big ghost leaving and coming back within a round trip respawns with its new values, then despawns after a leave), the existing replication suites (`replication.test.ts`, `replicationInterest.test.ts`, `replicationStructure.test.ts`, `sessionWebSocket.test.ts`, adjusted where they measured spawns in snapshots). **Not built:** reading late state parts (open question in the record); a separate spawn budget. |

| [0405](../decisions/0405-late-snapshot-parts-are-read-in-tick-order-and-acked.md) Late snapshot parts read and acked | Done | **Client** `src/engine/net/replication/client.ts`: `receive` reads a part of any tick within `BASELINE_WINDOW` not read yet (parts read kept per tick); `read` decodes into a staging list and keeps the part whole or not at all, stale per ghost (`ClientGhost.layoutTick`, structure tick, gone) or with a dropped baseline (late parts only); `fileState` files states in tick order; acks name the newest tick and late ticks, each repeated by `LATE_ACK_REPEATS` acks. **Server** `server.ts`: ack reading takes up to `MAX_ACK_TICKS` (`wire.ts`) ticks; `acked` accepts older ticks still in its history, baselines only move forward, records kept for the window. **Protocol** 7 (`net/session.ts`). **Tests** `src/engine/tests/replicationParts.test.ts` "late state parts are read in tick order (ADR 0403)": every-tick big changes keep updating and never go back, then converge; lifecycle messages every tick don't starve it; late parts from before a relayout are dropped and it converges. Safety net: the existing full-state fallback past `BASELINE_WINDOW`; no separate refresh path. |
| [0406](../decisions/0406-the-desktop-editor-builds-desktop-apps-with-the-hosts-toolchain.md) Desktop apps from the editor with the host's toolchain | Partial | **Shell** (`src-tauri/src/game_build.rs`, registered in `lib.rs`): `myco_build_toolchain` probes cargo, rustc, CMake, a C compiler (vswhere/MSVC, xcode-select, cc), `cargo tauri` and npx, with `~/.cargo/bin` and Homebrew appended to PATH; `myco_build_desktop_app` runs only `tauri icon` or `tauri build` (through `cargo tauri` or `npx --yes @tauri-apps/cli@2`) in a folder holding `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`, streaming output over a channel; Rust unit tests for the command lines. **Assessment** (`src/export/toolchain.ts`): ready + CLI, or the missing tools with URLs. **Editor**: `exportDesktopProject` (`exportBuild.ts`) writes `web/`, `src-tauri/` (gamepad.rs bundled `?raw`), `app-icon.png/svg` and README through the platform FileSystem, keeping `src-tauri/target/`; `hostToolchain.ts` wraps the commands; Build / Export probes on open and either builds (log of the last 300 lines, Show Folder on the installers) or says the toolchain is missing, with Check Again and Write Project. Tests: `toolchain.test.ts`, `exportBuild.test.ts`. Not yet: run end to end in a built desktop editor, cancelling a build, probing WebKitGTK on Linux, an e2e spec (Tauri-only). |
| [0407](../decisions/0407-user-docs-live-in-the-repo-and-ci-publishes-them-to-the-github-wiki.md) User docs → GitHub wiki | Partial | The guides moved from `docs/guide/` to `docs/wiki/guide/` (links across `docs/`, code comments and `tools/check-doc-links.mjs` rewritten); new `docs/wiki/Home.md`, `FAQ.md`, `News.md`, `_Sidebar.md`, `_Footer.md`. `tools/build-wiki.mjs` (`npm run docs:wiki`) builds the flat wiki: page names from headings, links rewritten, links leaving `docs/wiki/` made GitHub URLs, a TypeDoc + `typedoc-plugin-markdown` API reference of `src/myco.ts` (`typedoc.json`) as `API-<Kind>-<Name>` pages, and broken links or anchors fail it; the logic is `tools/wiki-pages.mjs`, unit-tested by `tools/wiki-pages.test.mjs` (`npm run test:tools`). `.github/workflows/wiki.yml` checks it on PRs and publishes on pushes to `master`, refusing to overwrite hand edits unless re-run with `overwrite`. The editor's Help > Documentation (`DOCS_URL`), `README.md` and the audio fallback warning point at the wiki. **Not verified:** the workflow hasn't run on GitHub yet (whether `GITHUB_TOKEN` can push to the wiki, the hand-edit guard); a local build writes 649 pages (611 API) with no problems. News dates before 2026-11-17 are approximate (taken from when each guide page was added). |
| [0408](../decisions/0408-the-studio-opens-a-takes-folder-without-a-project.md) Takes folder without a project, plain take files | Partial | **Plain files**: `capture/save.ts` writes `<storage root>/takes/*.sporesample` and `captures/*.sporesample` through the `FileSystem` (`SaveFiles` seam), newest 16 auto-named per folder; `trigger.ts` names the leaf. **Takes folder**: `src/studio/takesFolder.ts` (choice, default `<app data>/takes`, added takes); `openTakesFolder`/`isTakesFolder` (`editor/projects.ts`); `StudioProjects.openTakes`; `StudioOpenRequest.takesFolder`/`follow`. **Shell**: a takes folder offers Take and Profiler only (`TAKES_FOLDER_SCREENS`, `StudioHeader.onlyScreens`); Take saves into its root and shows Takes save to … with Change… (`BuildTakeControls.into`). **Live**: `shell/liveConnect.ts` opens the takes folder on the Profiler when a build connects with no project (or after Connect to Game…), and `followBuildWhenShown` starts the follow. **Recorded takes**: `listAllTakes` (project + loose takes), Project/Recording badges, a ⋯ menu with the path, Open Folder Location and Remove from list, the Live session folder line with Change…/Use default; the launcher adds dropped `.sporesample` files, and `lib.rs` allows the dropped take's folder in the fs scope. **Studio owns the build's takes**: the audio topic's `take` subscription (`debugLink/takeState.ts`, `AudioLinkRecord.takeState`) claims takes (`capture/takeOwnership.ts`, ADR 0417), so F10 stops without saving for the player, and each take that stops while it's open gets a backup copy in app data on Tauri (`topic.ts` `backupTake`, the auto-named rotation); `BuildTakeRecorder.watch` follows the game's starts and stops and saves a stopped take (`autoSave`); `take/buildTakes.ts` keeps the recorder at page level. A saved take goes into the Take or Profiler route (`lastBuildTake`); both screens open it once the build disconnects, so the recording just made stays on screen. **Browser**: an in-memory takes folder for the live session; `saveFileAs` (save dialog or download) for each take. Tests: `save.test.ts`, `trigger.test.ts` (F10 handed off, the take subscription), `topic.test.ts`, `recentTakes.test.ts`, `recordFromBuild.test.ts` (game-stopped takes), `liveConnect.test.ts`. Not yet: run in the desktop app (drag and drop, the folder dialog, a live build), an e2e spec. |
| [0409](../decisions/0409-recordings-are-one-sporesample-file-in-a-recordings-folder.md) One `.sporesample`, one `recordings/` | Done | `RECORDING_SUFFIX`, `RECORDINGS_FOLDER` (`studio/screens/profiler/captureTree.ts`; `CAPTURES_FOLDER` and `TAKES_FOLDER` point at it); `save.ts` writes both kinds to `<storage root>/recordings/`; the editor's record toggles write `recordings/take_NN.sporesample` and `capture_NN.sporesample`; both Studio trees list every recording; `takeScreen` opens one without the take flag as `noTake` ("No take data in this capture", Open in Profiler); `studioScreenFor` sends recordings to Take; the takes folder defaults to `<app data>/recordings`; the old extensions are gone. Not yet: the Spatial and animation take pickers still list profile-only recordings. |
| [0410](../decisions/0410-the-studio-downloads-a-connected-builds-audio-for-replays-without-a-project.md) Build audio for replays without the project | Partial | **Build**: `debugLink/buildAudio.ts` (`buildAudioIndex`: JSON files with `fnv1a` hashes, loose sounds, packages and streamed media from the media table; `buildFileBytes`), requests `build.audio` and `build.file` in `topic.ts`, uploads from `BUILD_FILE_UPLOAD_FIRST_ID` (`createTakeUploads` takes a first id and passes params to its source); `currentAudioHash` (take.ts), `captureProject` (capture.ts), `capture/fnv1a.ts`. **Studio**: `runtime/buildAudioSets.ts`: `downloadBuildAudio` (on by default; the Connect dialog's option with its warning, `ConnectDialog` `children`), `downloadBuildAudioSet` into `<takes folder>/.build-audio/<project>-<audioHash>/` (`set.json` last, unchanged files skipped), `installBuildAudioDownloads` with progress in the status line, `mountBuildAudioFor`/`unmountBuildAudio` (an asset source, the media table, the runtime on the set's audio), called by `takeScreen` for a take in a takes folder; Profile this replay is offered with a set mounted. | [0411](../decisions/0411-a-connected-studio-is-live-and-one-recording-shows-on-both-screens.md) Live while connected, one recording for both screens | Partial | **Build**: `takeLive` subscription (`debugLink/takeLive.ts`; records `takeLiveStart`/`takeLiveLog`/`takeLiveEmitters`/`takeLiveEnd`; the recorder's `readLog`), `take.frames` request (`setTakeFrameWidth`, 64..1920; an oversized picture narrows the next ones). **Studio**: `runtime/liveTake.ts` (`TakeLiveStream`, `liveTake`), `runtime/takePictures.ts` (the Connect dialog's width, sent on connect and change); `installProfilerLive` (always live, Rejoin); `refuseWhileLive` (saved recordings wait while connected); Take shows the live take (`LiveTakeView`); opening a recording opens it on both screens; the Profiler has `ReplayTransport` (inactive while recording); Record session and the Linked/Disconnected badge (`GameSessionSlot.tsx`, `RecordSession`); the Connect dialog's takes folder line and picture width; `audioProject`/`mountedAudioProject` (Playlists, the Mixer and the Take Inspector read the mounted build audio, read-only; `TAKES_FOLDER_AUDIO_SCREENS`), `openMixer`; no download with a project open; the take receiver skips a chunk delivered again. Tests: `audioLinkClient.test.ts` (scripted peer over the build's real uploads, and the loopback build), `buildSession.test.ts` (no React), `takeOwnership.test.ts`, `trigger.test.ts`, `assembler.test.ts`, `format.test.ts`, `takeLive.test.ts`, `linkSmoke`, `op1.walk`; e2e `debuglink-audio.spec.ts` uploads through the client. Not covered: the screens' live-or-file rendering and the routing of a kept take (e2e or UI test); `installBuildAudioDownloads` is still its own effect. |

| [0421](../decisions/0421-game-ui-is-built-only-from-ui-documents.md) Game UI only from UI documents | Done | **Removed:** `engine.Text` (`render/components.ts`; its Babylon drawing in `render/babylon/ui.ts` and `uiFeature.ts`, the `rect` drawn shape and `PICK_ORDER.screen` its scene-view picking used, its plugin registration and `myco` export) and `engine.LocalizedText` (`ui/LocalizedText.ts` and its `engine.LocalizedText` system). Key gathering (`localisation/exchange/gather.ts`) reads no scenes or prefabs: no `scene` key-use kind, no unkeyed scene text. **Added:** `UIText.textOutlineColor` (Color or null) and `textOutlineWidth` (UI px), stylesheet `text-outline-color`/`text-outline-width`, inherited (`INHERITED_FIELDS`); drawn as Babylon GUI `outlineColor`/`outlineWidth` (stroked twice the width, scaled with the canvas like the font, the redrawn rectangle widened by the outline so clearing text leaves none behind). Not part of the text run (0271): it doesn't change layout. `SubtitleDisplay`'s built-in document has a `<Canvas mode="screen" sort-order="1000">` root; the stylesheet wrapper is a root `<Use>` plus `<Style>`; a replacement document brings its own `<Canvas>`. With it in the same build: `addComponent` of a UI type throws and saved UI components fail to load (`GameObject`, scene loader), the editor's Create menu offers only UI Document and Add Component no UI types, and the samples' `Text` labels, Pirates' hand-built menu and HUD are documents. **Refusal:** `GameObject.addBehavior`/`addComponent`/`addChildBehavior` throw for the six UI types (matched by registered id up the prototype chain, so subclasses too); `UIDocument.build()` creates elements through `World._addComponent`, the only path. `SceneLoader` fails a saved UI component, `engine.Text` or `engine.LocalizedText` (by id or bare name) in scenes, prefabs and `addComponent` overrides with the file, node path and 0421, through `problem()`. `UIControlMapper` builds from built-in documents (`@myco/ui/control-mapper*.ui.xml`, its own canvas document) instead of components in code. Engine UI tests build from inline markup (`engine/tests/uiMarkup.ts`). **Editor:** `nodeKinds.ts` lists only UI Document under UI; `componentEdits.ts` leaves the UI types out of Add Component and refuses them (`whyNotAddable`). **Samples:** Flappy, Arena, Sound Lab and Navigation HUD text in `ui/*.ui.xml`/`.ui.css` with outlines; Pirates' menu and HUD (`ui/hud.ui.xml`, `ui.list` rows for the leaderboard, results and minimap layers). Guides: [UI](../wiki/guide/ui.md) (documents only, `text-outline-*`), [Input](../wiki/guide/input.md). Tests: `engine/tests/ui.test.ts` (the refusals: add methods, subclasses, scene, prefab and override loads, removed types), `editor/componentEdits.test.ts`, `editor/uiCreate.test.ts`, `engine/tests/babylonUI.test.ts` (outline), `uiDocument.test.ts` (outline cascade and inheritance), `uiFeature.test.ts`, `localisation/exchange/gather.test.ts`, `plugins/subtitles/runtime/subtitles.test.ts`. **0423 wave D (2026-11-26):** `UIDocument` removed: UI components are made only by the UI host (a `UIView`'s render); the refusal message names UIView only (`UI_FROM_VIEWS`, `_viewOnlyComponentId`); a saved `engine.UIDocument` (by id or bare name) fails to load naming the file, the node path and 0423 (`SceneLoader`'s `RETIRED_DOCUMENTS`), with no migration; the Create menu's UI section is **UI View** (a node with a `UIView` on a new component from the `svelte` kind's `create`), and Add Component's refusal points at a UIView's component. Engine UI tests build through `engine/tests/uiMarkup.ts`, now a `UIView` mounting `plugins/ui/runtime/testComponents/Tree.svelte` (a generic component rendering markup given as data through the real renderer and host). |
| [0422](../decisions/0422-the-ui-core-grows-toward-web-css-layout-paint-transforms-animation-and-selectors-in-waves.md) UI core toward web CSS | Partial | **Verification (End, all five waves):** `tests/e2e/ui-css.spec.ts` captures one Svelte test component per wave (layout; paint; transforms with skew/matrix; a transition and `@keyframes` sampled at fixed times by stepping the paused game; selectors, `calc()` and canvas `@media` across a resize) and the 0428/0429/0430 features (forms, inline runs and `{@html}`, filters and inline SVG) in Play mode, compared with committed baselines (`ui-css.spec.ts-snapshots/`, Playwright's `toHaveScreenshot`, 1% of pixels; Linux, software GL, device scale 1), each with public-API checks (`UIView.query`, `bounds()`). Frame time (`@slow`, Stats overlay Update + Render, views on minus views off, medians): control mapper 2.4–3.3 ms a frame (World 8.1–8.8 ms with it, 4.7–5.7 without), Pirates HUD in a bot match 2.3–5.4 ms (World 19.0–20.8 ms with it, 14.0–17.3 without), on a 4-core cloud container with software GL; tripwires 8 and 14 ms. No earlier baseline exists to compare against (commit 2286a9d7's documents can't run since 0423 wave D), so these are the first numbers. **Wave 1 (layout) built.** `UIElement` (`ui/components.ts`, under `// 0422 wave 1: layout`): per-side `paddingTop/Right/Bottom/Left` and `marginTop/…` (null: the all-sides `padding`/`margin`), `minWidth/minHeight/maxWidth/maxHeight`, `flexWrap`, `alignSelf`, `alignContent`, `flexShrink` (null: the kind's), `flexBasis`, `aspectRatio`, `overflow` (`visible`/`hidden`), `zIndex`; `UIPanel` (and buttons, input fields) `borderTopWidth/…` (null: `borderWidth`; borders exist only on panels, as `borderWidth` does). Shorthands in `document/stylesheet.ts` (its shorthand section) expand to longhands in the cascade, in rules and attributes: `padding`/`margin`/`border-width` 1–4 values (one value sets the all-sides field and clears the sides), `border: width solid\|none colour`, `flex: grow shrink basis` with CSS's one/two-value forms; `aspect-ratio: 16 / 9`. Yoga gets every field (`UICore.applyProperties`). `overflow: hidden` clips hit-testing, `pickAll` and `cornersOf` (new `clip` corners, which the editor's outline draws) to the padding box, drawing through a clip `Rectangle` (`clipChildren`, rounded inner corners) per clipper in `render/babylon/ui.ts`; navigation (0340) skips elements clipped away and searches the drawn part of the rest. `zIndex` orders drawing and hits among siblings, then document order; a non-zero `zIndex` or `overflow: hidden` groups descendants (CSS stacking context); `elements()` stays in tree order with `order` the draw order, and `UILaidOutElement` adds `clip`, `clipper`, `childClip`. Unequal border sides draw as four edge rectangles. Tests: `tests/uiLayoutWave1.test.ts`, `tests/babylonUI.test.ts`. **Content sizes as CSS (2026-11-27):** a plain text that is empty once its white space collapses measures 0 × 0, as an empty block (inputs, text areas, selects and editable text keep a line); a box with children gets a minimum width and height of 0.001 px (`CONTAINER_MIN_SIZE`, on top of the author's), because Yoga gives a box it measures at an available size of 0 or less the whole available size on the other axis instead of its content's (a shrink-to-fit column of empty lines pushed the Pirates HUD's bars off screen). A stopgap until the UI core moves from Yoga to Taffy. Tests: `uiLayoutWave1.test.ts` ("content sizes where Yoga's shortcuts differ from CSS"). **Missing (wave 1):** the inspector's value-source label for a shorthand (the source is already the shorthand's declaration), margin/padding `auto` and `%`; border styles other than solid. **Waves 2–5** (paint, transforms, animation, selectors and values): not built here. |
| [0422](../decisions/0422-the-ui-core-grows-toward-web-css-layout-paint-transforms-animation-and-selectors-in-waves.md) UI core toward web CSS: wave 2, paint | Partial (wave 2 built) | **Wave 2 (paint):** `UIPanel` fields `boxShadowColor`/`boxShadowOffsetX`/`boxShadowOffsetY`/`boxShadowBlur`, `backgroundImage` (CSS text), `borderTopLeftRadius`/`borderTopRightRadius`/`borderBottomRightRadius`/`borderBottomLeftRadius` (-1: take `borderRadius`); `UIText` fields `textShadowColor`/`textShadowOffsetX`/`textShadowOffsetY`/`textShadowBlur` (inherited). A shadow is several typed fields, not a structured value, so the Inspector edits them and wave 4 can animate them as numbers and colours. Values: `ui/document/paintValues.ts` (one shadow; `url()`, `linear-gradient()` with an angle or side, `radial-gradient()` as a circle at the centre, stops with `%` positions filled in as CSS; one to four corner radii; the gradient line and circle). `parseColor` moved to `ui/document/cssColor.ts` (re-exported by `stylesheet.ts`). Cascade (`stylesheet.ts`, paint section): `box-shadow`, `text-shadow` and `border-radius` are shorthands expanded to those fields from rules and attributes (the value source names the shorthand's declaration); `backgroundImage` is checked and its `url()` resolved against the file; bad values are left out and warned once. Drawing (`render/babylon/ui.ts`): a `PaintedRectangle` sets `cornerRadiusX..W` (by Rectangle's drawing X top-left, Y top-right, Z bottom-right, W bottom-left; its declarations' comments swap Z and W), `shadowOffsetX/Y`/`shadowBlur`/`shadowColor` scaled by the canvas (Canvas2D shadows ignore the transform), a `LinearGradient`/`RadialGradient` as `backgroundGradient` rebuilt for the box it's drawn in, and a `url()` as an `Image` child; the redrawn rectangle grows to clear a shadow that moved or shrank. Text shadows go on the TextBlock. Tests: `tests/uiPaint.test.ts` (values, cascade, built documents), `tests/babylonUI.test.ts` (paint on Babylon GUI). Capture and frame time: done (End; see the wave 1 row). **Missing (other waves):** layout (1, built separately), transforms (3, built separately), animation (4), selectors and values (5). |
| [0422](../decisions/0422-the-ui-core-grows-toward-web-css-layout-paint-transforms-animation-and-selectors-in-waves.md) UI core toward web CSS: wave 3, transforms | Partial (wave 3 built) | **Wave 3 (transforms):** `UIElement` fields (`ui/components.ts`, under `// 0422 wave 3`) `translateX`/`translateY` (px or % of the element's own box, default `"0"`), `scaleX`/`scaleY` (default 1), `rotate` (degrees, clockwise), `transformOriginX`/`transformOriginY` (default `"50%"`). The one transform module `ui/transform2d.ts` (pure): an element's own affine `T(origin)·T(translate)·R·S·T(−origin)` (CSS's individual-property order), composition with its ancestors', inversion, decomposition (rotation, scales, skew), transformed corners and bounds. Values: `ui/document/transformValues.ts` (`transform` lists folded into the fields, refusing a skew or a % translate after a rotation (**the skew refusal is lifted by 0430**: such lists are held in `transformMatrix`); `translate`, `scale`, `rotate` angles in deg/turn/rad/grad; `transform-origin` keywords, px, %). Cascade (`ui/document/cascade.ts`, transform section): `transform`, `translate`, `scale` and `transform-origin` are typed shorthands beside the paint ones, in rules and attributes; `rotate` and the length fields are checked as they're set; bad values warn once. The `translate` attribute stays the localisation marker (0356). UI core (`ui/UICore.ts`): after layout (Yoga unchanged) each element's whole transform and its inverse; `hitTest`, `pickAll` and routing invert it; clips become a chain of (padding box, clipper's inverse) steps, merged while untransformed; `cornersOf` returns the drawn corners, `layoutPoints` (ancestors' transforms only) and the clip in the element's frame; `UILaidOutElement` adds `transform` and `bounds` (the drawn, clipped axis-aligned box). Navigation (0340) searches `bounds`, so transformed boxes' centres. Drawing (`render/babylon/ui.ts`): controls stay flat; each gets `inverse(clipper's) · its own` as `leftInPixels/topInPixels` of its transformed corner plus `rotation`/`scaleX`/`scaleY` about its top-left (`transformCenterX/Y` 0); clip containers move with their clippers; shadows' offsets turn and blurs scale with the element; a skew (rotation inside an unequal scale) is drawn without it and warned once (**0430 draws it** through the full matrix; the warning is gone). Editor: the outline is the drawn box; resize handles work in the drawn frame (`uiHandleMath.ts` homography) and, on an element with its own transform, don't write `left`/`top`; moves use the parent's frame and `layoutPoints`. Tests: `tests/uiTransforms.test.ts` (module, values, cascade, hits, clipping, corners, navigation, picking), `tests/babylonUI.test.ts` (control properties after sync, nested and clipped), `editor/uiHandles.test.ts` (handles on a turned element). Capture and frame time: done (End; see the wave 1 row). **Missing (wave 3):** the hidden IME `<input>` placed at the field (it stays off-screen). (Drawing a skew: done by 0430.) |
| [0422](../decisions/0422-the-ui-core-grows-toward-web-css-layout-paint-transforms-animation-and-selectors-in-waves.md) UI core toward web CSS: wave 4, animation | Partial (wave 4 built) | **Wave 4 (animation), on the tween backend (0424):** values `ui/document/animationValues.ts` (times, timing functions, `transition`/`animation` shorthands and longhands as lists, CSS list-length rules); `@keyframes` in `document/stylesheet.ts` (own section: from/to/percentages and lists, declarations; `UIStylesheet.keyframes`, plain data, so Svelte styles compiled to rule data carry them); cascade (`document/cascade.ts`, Animation section): declarations ordered like fields (rules by specificity, attributes, inline style), parsed once per distinct value, carried under the non-field keys `TRANSITION_KEY`/`ANIMATION_KEY` of the resolved map; `transitionFields` (`all`, shorthands' longhands), `keyframeTracks` (per element: shorthands expand, variables substitute, per-keyframe `animation-timing-function`, last block of a name wins), `usesAnimation`. Runtime `ui/animation/`: `timingCurves.ts` (cubic-bezier as one exact Bézier segment of an engine curve, steps as step keys, reversed curves `1 − f(1 − x)`), `animatedValues.ts` (numbers, px/% lengths with a mixed pair jumping at the midpoint, colours in linear RGB, none colours as transparent, unset sides/corners from their all-sides field, discrete values at the midpoint), `UIAnimator.ts` (one per World, one backend from `openTweenBackend()`; the `engine.UIAnimation` late-update system after `engine.UIDocumentSync`/`engine.UIHostSync`, outside any World's animation evaluate-to-apply span, `PASS_NORMAL` with tweens on `TIME_UNSCALED`; each run's clock, delays, iterations, direction, fill, play state and ends kept host-side; a segment starting in a frame is a tween whose delay places its start exactly, eased through a curve loaded once per function and direction; a segment entered part-way (negative delay, resume, backend still loading, fractional last iteration) evaluated from the same curve in TS; a script's write wins over the field's transition/animation). `UIDocument` and `UIHostTree` hand each restyle's writes to it; `UIDocument.ready` waits for the backend when its sheets animate. Events `transitionend`, `animationstart`, `animationiteration`, `animationend` (with `propertyName`, `animationName`, `elapsedTime`) one frame later through `UIDocumentElement.on` (and the `UIEvents` buffer) and `UIHostElement.on` (`UIHostEvent` grew the three detail fields). Editor: value sources show `animated` (Transition/Animation over the underlying origin). Tests: `tests/uiAnimation.test.ts` (values, parser, cascade, curves against CSS's cubic-bezier and steps definitions, documents and hosted elements frame by frame on both backends: progress, easing computed by the backend, delays, interrupts, script override, colours, lengths, transforms, shadows, sides, corners, unscaled time, iterations, alternate, fill, play state, negative delay, fractional count, steps, events), `tests/uiAnimationPerf.test.ts` (500 elements × 2 transitions, medians: ~1 ms/frame, ~17 ms for the frame starting all 1,000, baseline 9 ms; headless), `editor/uiAnimationSources.test.ts`. **Missing (wave 4):** Svelte `ontransitionend`/`onanimation*` attributes (the renderer's event map in `src/plugins/ui/svelte/renderer.ts` needs the four names; `UIHostElement.on` works), `UIView.ready` waiting for the backend (its first animations run in TS until the next segment), inherited values passed on mid-transition, CSS's reversing-shortening of interrupted transitions, live changes to a running animation's duration/timing, `transition-behavior`/`linear()`. Capture (sampled half way and past the end by stepping) and frame time: done (End; see the wave 1 row). |
| [0422](../decisions/0422-the-ui-core-grows-toward-web-css-layout-paint-transforms-animation-and-selectors-in-waves.md) UI core toward web CSS: wave 5, selectors and values | Partial (wave 5 built) | **Wave 5 (selectors and values):** rule data (`ui/document/types.ts`): a compound's optional `not` (selector lists), `nth` (`{a, b, last?, ofType?, of?}` for `:first-child`/`:last-child`/`:only-child`/`:nth-child(an+b [of S])`/`:nth-last-child()` and the `-of-type` forms), `attributes` (`[a]`, `=`, `~=`, `\|=`, `^=`, `$=`, `*=`, `i` flag), `empty`, `root`; combinators `+` and `~`; `StyleRule.media` (query lists per `@media` level). Readers: `document/selectorQuery.ts` (queries, and the stylesheet parser's attribute selectors and wave 5 pseudo-classes through `parseCompoundText`: the same data both ways), `document/mediaQuery.ts` (hand-written media query lists: `width`/`height`/`aspect-ratio` with `min-`/`max-`/ranges, `orientation`, `and`/`or`/`not`, media types; evaluation, unknown features never match), `document/cssMath.ts` (`calc()`/`min()`/`max()`/`clamp()`, nesting; px-only to a value, mixed % and px to `calc(<p>% + <q>px)`). Cascade (`document/cascade.ts`): matching against `StyleTarget.children`/`content`/`attributes`/root `media` (positions count child elements, not text), `:root` as a pseudo-class, specificity (`selectorSpecificity`), `usesStructure`/`usesMedia`, math after `var()` in every declaration, attribute, transition and keyframe, mixed lengths kept in length fields. `@media` in `stylesheet.ts` (nested; `@keyframes` inside refused). UI host (`ui/host/UIHost.ts`) and `UIDocument`: lazy targets (children, text, attributes as written: markup attributes; the renderer passes Svelte elements' own, `data-*`/`aria-*`/HTML-only included, as `create`/`setAttributes`' `written`), restyles of the parent's subtree on add/remove/move and on class/attribute changes when the styles use structure, the root's canvas size from the last layout re-matching `@media`; with structural selectors, elements made since styles were last resolved and the first canvas size restyle as first styles (no transitions); documents restyle once after building and binding, which settles the tree (a script's class before the first frame transitions). Layout (`ui/layout.ts`, `UICore.applyProperties`): mixed lengths resolve against the parent's content box (`contentSize`; `flex-basis` along the parent's direction) and the canvas lays out once more where that changed (`Entry.calcBasis`). Svelte (`plugins/ui/svelte/globalTargets.ts`): the units ruling, the window target is the first view's canvas in UI pixels (`uiHostCanvas`): `innerWidth`/`innerHeight`/`outer*`, `devicePixelRatio` as device pixels per UI pixel, `resize`; `matchMedia` → `UIMediaQueryList` (a global target, `change` once a frame while listened to, `onchange`, `addListener`); UI code's `svelte/reactivity` is `plugins/ui/svelte/reactivity.ts` (Svelte's with `MediaQuery` on the renderer's window; `runtimeModules.ts`, `projectCode/uiBundle.ts` via `UI_REACTIVITY_SOURCE`, `svelteUI()` in `vite.shared.ts`). Tests: `engine/tests/uiSelectors.test.ts` (matching, specificity, calc, `@media` parsing and matching), `engine/tests/uiSelectorQuery.test.ts` (query ↔ stylesheet data round trip, refusals), `engine/tests/uiDocument.test.ts` (list rows, attributes, `@media`, calc at layout), `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewSelectors.test.ts` (`testComponents/Structure.svelte`: keyed adds/removes/reorders, attribute changes, `:empty`, calc at layout and after a resize, `@media` and `MediaQuery` across resizes), `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewGlobals.test.ts` (UI-pixel window sizes and ratio). Conformance unchanged (1076 here; `async-unresolved-promise` fails on this machine at the wave's base too, a timing sample). Capture (across a canvas resize) and frame time: done (End; see the wave 1 row). **Missing (wave 5):** `:has()`; `em`/`rem`/viewport units in `calc()`; interpolating mixed calc lengths in transitions; `svelte/reactivity/window` still reads the global window; the renderer's own `querySelector` on nodes (`renderer.ts`) keeps its small grammar (`UIView.query` has the full one); the editor's class-move check (`uiDocumentEdits.ts`) matches a copied target, so it ignores structural selectors. |
| [0423](../decisions/0423-game-ui-is-authored-in-svelte-rendered-onto-the-ui-core.md) Game UI authored in Svelte, rendered onto the UI core | Done: waves A, B, C, D (UI documents retired) and F (what D removed, rebuilt for Svelte), with waves X2, Y2 and Y3; Svelte compatibility measured by the conformance ratchet below | **Wave A part 1 (the runtime):** `svelte` pinned at exactly 5.59.2. `patches/svelte+5.59.2.patch` adds the optional renderer hook `bind(node, name, get, set) → cleanup`; the compiler emits `$.bind_renderer` for element `bind:` under a custom renderer (still refused on `<svelte:window>`/`<svelte:document>`/`<svelte:body>`), a renderer without `bind` throws `renderer_bind_unsupported` when the binding is created (the compiler can't see the renderer), `bind:this` gives the renderer's node ([svelte-patch.md](svelte-patch.md), for upstream). The renderer (`plugins/ui/svelte/renderer.ts`, imported by compiled components as `myco/ui-renderer`): HTML tags → UI elements (`engine/ui/host/htmlTags.ts`; unknown tags warn once and are Panels), attributes → fields (HTML-only attributes stay on the node), `class`/`id`/`style` → cascade, text, `onclick`/`oninput`/`onchange`/`onsubmit`/`onfocus`/`onblur`/`onpointerenter`/`onpointerleave` from the interaction log (a handler gets a `UIViewEvent`; `UIEvent` stays UI documents'), `bind:value` on `input`, an Element subset on nodes. The UI core's external tree host (`engine/ui/host/UIHost.ts`, internal): create/setAttributes/setText/move/remove, restyle in the same frame, CSS from components (`css: 'external'`) and `:global` reaching every view in the World, editor picking (`uiHostElementOf`), systems `engine.UIHostEvents` (update), `engine.UIHostFlush` (late update, before `engine.UIDocumentSync`) and `engine.UIHostSync`. 0421 amended: a `UIView` render creates UI components; `addComponent` stays refused. `UIView` (`plugins/ui/runtime/UIView.ts`, public): `component` (`.svelte` asset), the canvas fields, makes the canvas root, reactive `props`, `ready`, `query`/`queryAll`, unmount on disable/removal, mounts again on a new component version (hot reload). Selectors: `:is()`/`:where()` (zero specificity), case-insensitive tags, the cascade matches the tag as written. Vitest compiles `.svelte`/`.svelte.ts` with `svelteUI()` (`vite.shared.ts`). Tests: `plugins/ui/runtime/uiView.test.ts`, `plugins/ui/svelte/sveltePatch.test.ts`, `engine/tests/uiSelectors.test.ts`. **Conformance baseline** at wave A (`npm run test:svelte-conformance`, not in `npm test`; Svelte 5.59.2 `runtime-runes` samples, ratchet in `tools/svelte-conformance/baseline.json`; the current numbers are under wave X2): 964 pass, 347 fail, 53 skip (73.5% of 1311 run). Failures: `on:` refused 111, assertion failed 74, rendered HTML differs 36, uses `document`/`window` 33, `transition:` refused 27, DOM API missing on nodes 11, `{@html}` refused 11, `<svelte:head>` refused 9, other runtime error 9, `snippet_renderer_mismatch` 8, `out:` 6, `in:` 4, `<svelte:window>` 3, `animate:` 2, `const_tag_invalid_reference` 1, `<svelte:document>` 1, `invalid_snippet_in_custom_renderer` 1. **Wave A part 2 (compiling):** the project code linker compiles UI code (`projectCode/compileUI.ts`): `.svelte` components, `.svelte.ts`/`.svelte.js` rune modules anywhere in the project (text project files now), and the `.css` files they import (each a module registering its rules; unimported `.css` never loads); `dev: true` in the memory target (editor), false in the build target. Svelte's runtime and the renderer are UI runtime modules (`projectCode/uiModules.ts`) every host provides from its own copy, never bundled as npm packages: the memory target shims the editor's copies, loaded on demand (`plugins/ui/svelte/runtimeModules.ts`); a game build bundles them into its own code, tree-shaken (0425, its row; as built here the player had fixed-name entries, `uiRuntimeEntries()`, now removed). A loaded component is its `.svelte` file's asset value (`Assets.setCompiledModules`, internal), in the editor after each link and in the player after its scripts load. Editor: UI files link with the scripts on open; saving a component, rune module or a `.css` a component names (Code panel, outside edit, New…) reloads, and mounted UIViews remount with their props; a compile error is a script problem (status line, `line N:`) and the component keeps its last version; the `svelte` kind has New Svelte Component (`ui/`); the Code panel edits `.svelte` as HTML and `.svelte.ts` as TypeScript, the TypeScript service has Svelte's declarations (runes) and `*.svelte` modules, and Play's type check covers `.svelte.ts` (not inside `.svelte`: no svelte-check). Export: `planWebBuild` takes `ui`, compiles with `dev: false`, ships the compiled modules and not the `.svelte`/`.svelte.ts` sources (`code/ui/Hud.svelte.js`; rune modules keep `.ts` in the module path). **Styles as data:** components' CSS and imported `.css` are parsed (css-tree) when they compile into `UIStylesheet` rule data written into the module; the UI host takes rule data only, and loads the cascade (`ui/document/cascade.ts`, split out of `stylesheet.ts`) and a parser-free selector reader for `query()` (`ui/document/selectorQuery.ts`) as one lazy chunk (`ui/host/hostSupport.ts`). Vitest's `svelteUI()` parses on module load instead. UI documents keep runtime parsing. **Sizes** (player build, gzip, beyond the player and `myco.js`): a Svelte-only game's UI 58.9 KB = Svelte runtime 45.8 KB (the whole internal client, with the entries' re-export lists: the prebuilt player's entries can't be tree-shaken per game) + renderer 4.4 KB + cascade and selector reader 8.7 KB (the cascade is shared with UI documents) + its rule data (the test shop's 9 rules: 0.66 KB gzip, 3x the CSS text); UI documents' path is 44.6 KB (parsers 37.3 KB + the same cascade); the base dropped 8 KB because the cascade left the parsers chunk's place in it. Tree-shaken per game, the runtime for a small HUD is 18.5 KB (≈32 KB in all), which needs the renderer built into each game's code (a follow-up). Tests: `editor/svelteUI.test.ts` (editor compile → UIView mount → hot reload, compile errors, New Svelte Component), `export/svelteExport.test.ts` (export compile: no compiler, no dev code, rule data; the compiled modules mount through the player's entry layout), `engine/tests/uiSelectorQuery.test.ts`. **Wave X2 (hooks without UI core dependencies, `t()`, the Element subset):** the patch adds the optional hooks `globalTarget(kind)` (`<svelte:window>`, `<svelte:document>`, `<svelte:body>`: events through `addEventListener`, bindings through `bind`, actions; `<svelte:head>` content goes into `globalTarget('head')` and `<title>` sets the document target's `title`; listeners on a global target are removed with their component) and `addStyles(hash, code)` (`css: 'injected'`, now honoured under a custom renderer), and lifts the refusal of legacy `on:` (it was already `$.event` → `addEventListener` with Svelte's modifier wrappers); missing hooks throw `renderer_global_target_unsupported`/`renderer_add_styles_unsupported` when first used ([svelte-patch.md](svelte-patch.md), one upstream PR per hook). The renderer: global targets (`plugins/ui/svelte/globalTargets.ts`) fed once a frame by a new host hook (`onUIHostFrame`, system `engine.UIHostFrame` after `engine.UIHostEvents`; only in play) from the device snapshot after UI routing: `keydown`/`keyup` (DOM `key`/`code`, modifiers), `pointerdown`/`pointerup`/`mousedown`/`mouseup` outside UI, `pointermove`/`mousemove`, `wheel`, body → document → window until stopped; `resize` (the drawing canvas's CSS size: `UILayoutSource.viewport`, `UICore.viewport`; since 0422 wave 5 the first view's canvas in UI pixels, that row), `online`/`offline`, `visibilitychange`; read-only bindings `innerWidth`/`innerHeight`/`outerWidth`/`outerHeight`/`devicePixelRatio`/`online`/`scrollX`/`scrollY` and `activeElement` (the UI's focus, `uiHostFocused`)/`visibilityState`/`fullscreenElement`/`pointerLockElement`; `document.title` sets the page's title (the desktop window's too: no platform title API exists for games, the webview's title is used); other head content warns once and isn't built. Injected CSS is parsed when it compiles (`compileSvelteUI` reads the `$$css` literal; `injectStyles(hash, data)` registers it, the `addStyles` hook adds it). Elements keep listeners of any type (custom events through `dispatchEvent`, `once`); a removed `<video>`/`<audio>` drops its listeners as Svelte's DOM runtime does. **Element subset** on nodes: `dataset`, `style` by camel-case property, `getAttributeNames`, `closest`, `matches`, `contains`, `parentNode`, `firstChild`/`lastChild`/`nextSibling`/`previousSibling`, `firstElementChild`/`lastElementChild`/`nextElementSibling`/`previousElementSibling`/`childElementCount`, `isConnected`, `ownerDocument`, and `getBoundingClientRect` from `UIHostElement.bounds()` (the transformed box's axis-aligned bounds in canvas UI pixels, unclipped; 0422 wave 3). **`t(key, args)`** from "myco" (`engine/localisation/t.ts`): `Locale.t`, plus a reactive read the renderer installs (`createSubscriber` over `onLocaleEpoch`, a new internal epoch listener in `Locale.ts`), so components follow language switches and table edits. Tests: `plugins/ui/svelte/sveltePatch.test.ts` (compile output, runtime hooks, clear errors), `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewGlobals.test.ts` (window sizes and `resize`, keys body → document → window, `activeElement`, `<title>`, injected CSS, `t()` after `setLanguage`, the Element subset, `on:` modifiers and cleanup). **Conformance now:** 1077 pass, 234 fail, 53 skip (82.2% of 1311 run; was 964, 73.5%); the harness passes the renderer's window target as a test's `window` argument and handles injected CSS. Failures: assertion failed 76, uses `document`/`window` 39 (mostly configs reading the global `window`/`document`, which stay undefined in the harness: engine code takes a defined `window` for a browser), rendered HTML differs 38, `transition:` refused 28, `{@html}` refused 12, DOM API missing on nodes 10, other runtime error 9, `snippet_renderer_mismatch` 8, `out:` 6, `in:` 4, `animate:` 2, `const_tag_invalid_reference` 1, `invalid_snippet_in_custom_renderer` 1. Most of the assertion and HTML failures are event propagation and delegation (Y3), form elements and `select` (Z2), SVG/MathML namespaces, `$inspect` stack frames and `svelte-meta` details. **Wave Y2 (transitions and `animate:`):** the patch adds the optional hooks `animate(node, keyframes, { duration, fill }) → { currentTime, onfinish, onframe, cancel() }` and `measure(node) → { box, clientWidth/Height, style }` and lifts the refusal of `transition:`, `in:`, `out:` and `animate:`; the runtime plays keyframes through `animate` instead of `element.animate`, runs `tick` transitions from the handle's `onframe` instead of `requestAnimationFrame`, and `animate:` (`fix()` through the `style` attribute), `svelte/transition` and `svelte/animate` read geometry through `measure` (the transition function runs with its renderer current, deferred ones too); missing hooks throw `renderer_animate_unsupported`/`renderer_measure_unsupported` ([svelte-patch.md](svelte-patch.md)). Engine: `UIAnimator.play` (`ui/animation/UIAnimator.ts`) runs keyframe tracks as an element's own animation on the tween backend, per-segment tweens (the backends hold 256 sequences, 4,096 tweens), unscaled time, first keyframe shown the frame it starts (after a delay too), equal stretches merged, held until stopped, kept across restyles, `onFrame`/`onEnd` hooks after the frame's values, no CSS events; `UIHostElement.animate` converts CSS keyframes with the cascade's `keyframeValues` (now exported) and warns once per tag and property for what it can't animate; `UIHostElement.computedStyle()`; `UIHostTree.layoutNow()` restyles pending changes and lays the World out again (`UILayoutSource.relayout`, `UICore.relayout`) when a hosted tree changed since the last time that frame, so geometry read right after a change is current (the renderer's `getBoundingClientRect` uses it too). Renderer: `plugins/ui/svelte/animation.ts` (the two hooks), `getTotalLength()`/`getPointAtLength()` on SVG shapes (0430; 0, warned, elsewhere). `svelte/transition`: `fade`, `fly`, `scale`, `slide`, `crossfade` work; `blur` and `draw` work since 0430 (`blur` animates the filter fields; `draw` writes stroke dashes into an `<svg>`'s document each frame). `svelte/animate` `flip` works. `svelte/motion` (`Tween`, `Spring`) works on Svelte's own frame clock (`requestAnimationFrame`), not engine frames (importing it reads `matchMedia`; canvas `MediaQuery` is wave Y1's; both fixed in wave Z3). The renderer's inline `style` serialises as the DOM does (`a: 1; b: 2;`, `style=""` once emptied). Tests: `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewTransitions.test.ts`, `plugins/ui/svelte/sveltePatch.test.ts`. **Conformance now:** 1112 pass, 199 fail, 53 skip (84.8% of 1311 run; was 1077, 82.2%); the harness's `animate` is Svelte's test `Animation` stand-in moved onto the hook (`tools/svelte-conformance/animationStandIn.ts`), the renderer's `measure` stays. Failures: assertion failed 77, uses `document`/`window` 40, rendered HTML differs 40, `{@html}` refused 12, DOM API missing on nodes 10, other runtime error 10, `snippet_renderer_mismatch` 8, `const_tag_invalid_reference` 1, `invalid_snippet_in_custom_renderer` 1. Transition samples still failing (12 of 46): 6 read `HTMLElement`/`document` in their config, 3 expect jsdom's computed opacity `""` (0) where the renderer gives the element's opacity, `if-transition-undefined` reads the global `name`, `async-branch-reintro` needs `:last-child` in `querySelector`, `async-transition-pending-boundary` uses `HTMLElement`. **Wave Y3 (0426 events, 0427 scrolling; their rows):** events propagate through the renderer's nodes (capture, target, bubble, then `<svelte:body>`/`<svelte:document>`/`<svelte:window>`), `addEventListener` takes `capture`/`once`/`passive`, `dispatchEvent` propagates, the pointer, key, focus, drag and scroll events are sent, and nodes have the DOM's scrolling members. **Conformance after Y3 (on its branch, without Y1 and Y2):** 1086 pass, 225 fail, 53 skip (82.8% of 1311 run; +9: `event-attribute-capture`, `event-attribute-delegation`, `-delegation-4`, `-delegation-7`, `event-attribute-spread-capture`, `event-attribute-window`, `event-on-nested-root`, `event-prop-current-target`, `deferred-events-consistency-3`). `async-unresolved-promise` fails in some full runs under load and passes alone (timing; it failed before Y3 too) and stays in the baseline. Failures: assertion failed 72, uses `document`/`window` 40, `renderer_animate_unsupported` 37 (Y2's), rendered HTML differs 35, `{@html}` refused 12, DOM API missing on nodes 10, other runtime error 10, `snippet_renderer_mismatch` 8, `const_tag_invalid_reference` 1, `invalid_snippet_in_custom_renderer` 1. **Conformance with Y1, Y2 and Y3 merged:** 1121 pass, 190 fail, 53 skip (85.5% of 1311 run; exactly the union of Y2's 1112 and Y3's 1086 passing sets, nothing lost); `async-unresolved-promise` passed in this run and stays in the baseline (it flakes under load). Failures: assertion failed 72, uses `document`/`window` 40, rendered HTML differs 36, `{@html}` refused 12, DOM API missing on nodes 10, other runtime error 10, `snippet_renderer_mismatch` 8, `const_tag_invalid_reference` 1, `invalid_snippet_in_custom_renderer` 1. **Conformance with Z1 and Z2's 0428 and 0430 merged:** 1125 pass, 186 fail, 53 skip (85.8% of 1311 run; gained `attribute-spread-input`, `select-function-binding-derived`, `select-spread-option-selected` and `state-bind-group` with the form elements, nothing lost). Failures: assertion failed 68, uses `document`/`window` 40, rendered HTML differs 38, `{@html}` refused 12, DOM API missing on nodes 9, other runtime error 9, `snippet_renderer_mismatch` 8, `const_tag_invalid_reference` 1, `invalid_snippet_in_custom_renderer` 1. **Conformance with 0429 (Z2) merged:** 1130 pass, 181 fail, 53 skip (86.2% of 1311 run; gained `async-html-tag`, `each-dynamic-html`, `html-tag-empty`, `html-tag-escaping` and `hydration-html-changed-ignored` with the `html` hook, nothing lost; the harness registers the inline HTML parser as `compileSvelteUI` does, and a text element mirrors its rendered text through its runs). Failures: assertion failed 74, uses `document`/`window` 40, rendered HTML differs 38, DOM API missing on nodes 12, `snippet_renderer_mismatch` 8, other runtime error 7, `const_tag_invalid_reference` 1, `invalid_snippet_in_custom_renderer` 1; `{@html}` is no longer refused (the 4 `{@html}` samples still failing: an `<a>` namespace check, `contenteditable` on a `<div>` (text elements only, 0429) and two `svelte-meta` details). **Conformance after the engine round (2026-11-26):** 1274 pass, 35 fail, 55 skip (97.3% of 1309 run); **in scope: 1274 of 1278 (99.7%)**, 31 run out of scope. Class (a) is done (`TRIAGE.md` has each root cause): the 12 async/fork samples were the harness (Svelte's harness drops leftover batches with its batch module's `clear()` after each sample and empties logs after import; a worktree's symlinked Svelte sources gave fixtures a second runtime), a renderer selector parser that misread pseudo-classes (`querySelector`/`matches`/`closest` now use the cascade's grammar and matcher, `matchesUISelector`) and `insert` before a detached anchor (now nothing, as the DOM's `before()`); `UIView.exports` and `UIView.transformError` (public, coordinator decision; 0423 as built); HTML form defaults and reset in the renderer (`defaultValue`/`defaultChecked`/`defaultSelected` with dirty flags, a text area's content, Svelte's select `defaultValue`, `setUIDefaults` for the UI core's reset; `<input type="submit|reset|button">` is a button; `select.value` chooses at once; HTML's selectedness algorithm); form controls inside text stay controls; the Element subset's `focus()`/`blur()` send their events at once; a disabled control's `click()` does nothing; `muted` reaches media at once; an audio source's seek events come after the setting code; `inert` (a UI element field and the attribute) takes a subtree out of hit-testing, focus and navigation (0426's row). 4 need a second patch change (`TRIAGE.md` class c2: focused-binding batches passed to `bind`, a spread's first nullish `checked`, a select's first nullish `value`), verified with the changes applied at load time; `dynamic-component-transition`/`dynamic-if-component-transition` are jsdom-only (computed opacity ""). Tests: `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewFormDefaults.test.ts`, `uiView.test.ts` (exports, transformError), `uiViewElementSubset.test.ts` (selectors), `uiViewEvents.test.ts` and `engine/tests/uiEvents.test.ts` (inert). **Conformance after the patch round (2026-11-26):** 1241 pass, 68 fail, 55 skip (94.8% of 1309 run); **in scope: 1241 of 1280 (97.0%)**, 29 run out of scope. The 14 patch changes the triage wrote up are in `patches/svelte+5.59.2.patch` ([svelte-patch.md](svelte-patch.md), "Event delegation" and "Fixes to custom-renderer paths"): `createElement(name, namespace)`; `inert` set through the renderer in outros; event attributes delegated under a renderer as on the DOM (Svelte's own delegation through the renderer's listener on the mount target, path by `getParent`), so handler order and dev handler names match; handlers run with their renderer current and `mount()` without `renderer` defaults to it; `createRawSnippet` through the `html` hook (the inline HTML parser now keeps comments, and is imported for `createRawSnippet` too); `{@html}` dev locations; `<option>`'s nullish value and `<select multiple>`'s non-array warning (compiler); dev checks the renderer path skipped (snippet anchors, `invalid_default_snippet`, snippet stringification, array warnings). `svelte/motion`'s `prefersReducedMotion` needed none (Z3's resolution; the harness now does it too). Remaining: 39 engine or renderer gaps (class a), 12 jsdom-only and 17 out of scope. **Conformance after the triage (2026-11-26):** 1210 pass, 99 fail, 55 skip (92.4% of 1309 run); **in scope: 1210 of 1281 (94.5%)**, 28 run out of scope. Every sample that doesn't pass is classified in `tools/svelte-conformance/TRIAGE.md`: 41 engine or renderer gaps (open: async/fork scheduling 12, `UIView` lacks the root component's exports 3 and `mount`'s `transformError` 6, `defaultValue`/`defaultChecked` 4, focused selects and fields 3, `__svelte_meta` through components 2, others one or two each), 30 needing patch changes (14 written up there: namespaces in `createElement`, `inert` in outros, `createRawSnippet` through the `html` hook, `mount()` inside a component defaulting to the active renderer, root delegation order, dev checks and warnings the renderer path skips, `svelte/motion`'s import-time `MediaQuery`), 11 harness or jsdom-only expectations and 17 out of scope per 0423 (custom elements, component code using `document`/`window`, `document.head` markup, `<script>` in markup, `FormData`, Svelte's `hmr`); the last 28 are `baseline.json`'s `outOfScope` (with reasons), which the harness reads to report the in-scope rate. The triage fixed the harness where it misrepresented behaviour (configs get the page's `window`/`document` as `pageGlobals.ts`, Svelte's `raf` helper, `rootDir`, `console.log` returning undefined, a listener's exception reported as jsdom reports it, a fresh page per sample, `skip_async`, the engine's `svelte/reactivity`, class lists and inline styles compared as sets of tokens and declarations) and the renderer and UI core (see 0428's row for the click order; nodes gained `namespaceURI`, reflected attributes, `stepUp`/`stepDown`, `getElementsBy*`, `selectedOptions`, iterable selects, booleans written as the DOM writes them; tests: `src/plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewElementSubset.test.ts`). `SVELTE_CONFORMANCE_CACHE` moves a run's fixtures and output. **Not yet:** `inert` on outroing elements (DOM-only in the patch), element size bindings, waves C–D (the form elements are 0428's row). **Wave Z3 (Svelte's timing on the engine frame; 0431):** the patch adds the optional hooks `requestAnimationFrame(callback)` and `now()`: while a component mounted with such a renderer is mounted (released after its unmount and outros), Svelte's `raf` (`timing.js`, a stack of sources) uses them, so `loop`, `svelte/motion` (`Tween`, `Spring`, `tweened`, `spring`) and `tick` transitions run on the renderer's frames; a `tick` transition under such a renderer runs on `loop` as in the DOM (the `onframe` handle property stays for renderers without the hooks) ([svelte-patch.md](svelte-patch.md)). Renderer: `plugins/ui/svelte/timing.ts`: the clock is the active World's unscaled time in ms, summed per frame (monotonic across Worlds); callbacks run once in the next frame through `onUIHostFrame` (update phase, after UI events, before behaviors and long before the late UI sync), headless included, never in edit mode; the `animate` handle's `currentTime` is on that clock. So `tick` transitions' inline-style writes now show in the frame they're made (was: a frame late), and motion values show the frame they're computed. `svelte/motion`'s own `svelte/reactivity` import resolves to the canvas `MediaQuery` module (`vite.shared.ts`, `projectCode/uiBundle.ts`), so importing it no longer needs a browser `window` and `prefersReducedMotion` follows `UIPreferences` (0431's row: `prefers-reduced-motion`/`prefers-color-scheme` in canvas `@media`). The editor's built-in UI components load through `plugins/builtinUILoads.ts` and `loadProjectCode` awaits them (fixes an unhandled teardown error when a test file ended before the control mapper's component finished compiling).
