Tests: `sveltePatch.test.ts` (motion on a test renderer's frames and clock, released on unmount), `uiViewTransitions.test.ts` (`Tween`/`Spring` frame by frame on engine frames only, unscaled; a `tick` transition's inline style shown the same frame), `uiViewPreferences.test.ts`. **Wave B (Z1):** the built-in UI is Svelte: `UIControlMapper` (moved to `src/plugins/ui/runtime/UIControlMapper.ts`) and `SubtitleDisplay` mount a `UIView` of a built-in component (`@myco/ui/ControlMapper.svelte` from `src/plugins/ui/builtin/`, `@myco/ui/Subtitles.svelte` from `src/plugins/subtitles/runtime/SubtitleRows.svelte`) and pass what they show as props; their public API is unchanged except `SubtitleDisplay`'s `document`/`stylesheet`, which become `component` (a replacement component with the same props) and theme variables in any component's global rule. `projectCode/builtinUI.ts` lists the built-ins: the editor (dev server and build) and Vitest compile them with Vite's `svelteUI()` (each plugin's `builtinUI.ts`, which its editor half loads when it activates, and which the tests that use them import); a game build (`export/webBuild.ts`) compiles the ones a project uses (a scene, prefab, script or UI source naming the component) into its own code from the engine's files (`collectUIRuntimeFiles` serves their sources to the editor's export), so the player still carries no Svelte. `UIView._flush()` (internal) lets the mapper read its rendered buttons in the same frame. The mapper's view draws a nested canvas under a game's canvas (UIView always makes one), with that canvas's settings and `sortOrder + 1`. The samples (flappy, arena, sound-lab, pirates, the navigation HUD) are Svelte components with UIViews; pirates' screens take one state prop each, button callbacks and bound fields. Fixes: the cascade reads an inline style's custom properties (`style="--x: …"`, Svelte's CSS custom property props); the renderer keeps an empty Text's `text` attribute as written. `button:focus` matches (a button's pseudo-state has ADR 0340's focus, as every focusable control's does), and the mapper's focused border is `button:focus`. Not done: no sample text is localised (none was), the samples' Svelte UI in e2e specs is unrun. **Wave C (editor tooling, owner decision 1: static parts editable, dynamic parts view-only):** `editor/uiViews.ts` (the model, beside `uiDocuments.ts`): a view's rendered element is addressed by its template location, `uiv:<host>|<index>|<file>|<line>:<column>|<nth>` (`nth` for an `{#each}`'s or snippet's repeats), resolved by walking the view's tree for `UIHostElement.source`; hierarchy rows (`viewRows`), picking (`viewElementOfComponent`: a button's synthetic label picks the button; a view's canvas picks its node), and value sources (`viewFieldSources`: literal attribute or literal `style` with file and line, `expression` (a new `FieldOrigin` kind, view-only, shown as written), scoped and `:global` rules mapped back to the selector as written and its line in the component's `<style>` or the imported `.css`, inherited, text content, default, transition/animation). `whyViewOnly`: a child component's insides (another file), `{#each}` rows and snippet elements (from `parse()`), anything rendered more than once from one place, or a file that changed shape. `editor/uiEdit/svelteText.ts` (beside `markupText.ts`): edits on `parse(src, { modern: true })` spans (literal attribute set/insert/remove, a literal `style`'s declarations, literal text, insert/insert-after/duplicate with fresh ids/move/remove elements, `<style>` rules), refusing `{…}`; `svelte/compiler` loads on demand (`loadSvelteParser`, kicked by the first hosted tree). `editor/uiViewEdits.ts` (beside `uiDocumentEdits.ts`): fields, text, id, static class, Move to class (into the component's `<style>`), Create (HTML tags and 0428's form tags, into containers or after), duplicate, delete, move/reorder within the file; each a `WriteFiles` command on the scene's stack (undo/redo/History), the selection following the element's new location. Hot reload: `textFiles.ts` runs `onUICodeWritten` after writing UI code (and `flush()` waits for it); the session reloads the project's code quietly (`reloadScripts({ quiet })`). Wired in: Inspector (`ui/UIViewElementInspector.tsx`, sharing `UIElementInspector.tsx`'s field rows; sources open the Code panel at the line, documents' too now), Hierarchy rows (view-only ones marked) and row menu, Create menu ("Element in Svelte Component"), Delete/Ctrl+D, hierarchy drag (`uiElementDrag.ts`), viewport picking (`viewportSelection.ts`), outline, and scene-view handles (`uiHandleDrags.ts` now reaches both kinds through one `HandleSubject`). No engine or renderer change: the dev-mode locations the host already recorded were enough. Tests: `editor/uiViews.test.ts` (rows, picking, sources, view-only, edits with undo/redo and hot reload, create/duplicate/move/delete, Move to class, handles, menus, read-only and play refusals), `editor/uiEdit/svelteText.test.ts`. **Not yet (wave C):** a view of a child component can't edit that component's insides from the parent (open a view of it); Move to class doesn't warn about more specific rules (documents' does); `class:`/`style:` directives and spreads show as expressions only; a hierarchy drag can't reparent across `{#if}`/`{#each}` blocks (moves within one fragment); the Inspector types no `UIView` props (0423's open question). **Conformance after the second patch round (2026-11-26):** 1278 pass, 31 fail, 55 skip (97.6% of 1309 run); **in scope: 1278 of 1278 (100%)**, 31 run out of scope (every failure). `TRIAGE.md` class c2 is done: `patches/svelte+5.59.2.patch` passes `bind` a fifth argument `ownChange()` (the batches its own `set` ran in, as Svelte's DOM bindings keep them, so a focused field or select isn't rewritten from its own change's batch), keeps a spread's default for its first nullish `checked`/`value` under a renderer, and tells the renderer of a select's first nullish `value` ([svelte-patch.md](svelte-patch.md)); the renderer's `bind:value` shows a setter-changed value a tick after an `input`, focused or not, as Svelte's `bind_value`. Tests: `plugins/ui/svelte/sveltePatch.test.ts` (`ownChange`, the select's nullish value, the spread's default). **Wave D (2026-11-26): UI documents retired.** Removed: `engine/ui/UIDocument.ts` (the component, `UIDocumentElement`, `UIClassList`, `UIEvents`, `UIEvent`/`UIEventHandler`/`UIEventType`/`UIListOptions`, `loadUIDocumentSupport`, `engine.UIDocumentEvents`/`engine.UIDocumentSync`), `engine/ui/documentSource.ts`, `ui/document/markup.ts` and `parsers.ts`, `{name}` bindings (`parseBindings`/`hasBinding`, the cascade's bound-attribute skip, `boundValue`), `t:` key attributes (`keyAttributeField`, `isTextField`), `MarkupElement`'s document fields (`text`, `template`, spans, `from`, `inlineTag`) and `UIMarkup`/`ParseMarkup`, the `uidocument` asset field kind, `SceneManager`'s parser preload, the `myco` exports above (`UITag` stays, from `ui/document/types.ts`), and `UIHostEvent.item`. **Moved:** the frame's event dispatch is `engine.UIEventDispatch` (`ui/host/UIHost.ts`; after `engine.Lobby`/`engine.PredictionSmooth`, before `engine.InputFrame`, `engine.UIHostEvents` and `engine.UpdateScripts`, where documents' system was). **Added to the host:** node references by element id (`nav-up`/`nav-down`/`nav-left`/`nav-right`, `first-selected`), resolved after restyles and again when ids or the tree change (`resolveReferences`). **Loads:** `SceneLoader` fails a saved `engine.UIDocument`/`UIDocument` with the file, node path and 0423; the UI refusal message is `UI_FROM_VIEWS`. **Editor:** removed `uiDocuments.ts`, `uiDocumentEdits.ts`, `uiEdit/markupText.ts`, `uiEdit/cssText.ts`, the document Inspector (`UIElementInspector`), rows, menus, drags, handle subjects and selection keys (`ui:`), `plugins/ui/editor/uiDocumentAssets.ts` and the `uidocument` asset kind; the shared parts are `uiFieldSources.ts` (origins, `kebab`, `formatValue`, `attributeText`, `cssValue`, `NodeLookup`) and `ui/UIFieldSourceView.tsx` (field rows, `DraftInput`, `MoveToClass`); the edit gate's target is `{ kind: "ui" }`; Create > UI is **UI View** (`nodeKinds.ts`, `kindAssetCreator` → the `svelte` kind's `create`); the localisation tools that read or wrote markup are gone (see 0356's row). `htmlparser2` stays as an editor-only dependency (XLIFF, the conformance harness). **Tests re-pointed, not dropped:** `engine/tests/uiMarkup.ts` builds the same trees through a `UIView` mounting `plugins/ui/runtime/testComponents/Tree.svelte` (markup given as data, HTML tags; a `<Canvas>` root's attributes are the view's settings or the canvas's; `css` is parsed with `stylesheet.ts` and registered as a component's styles; `uiElement` adds one element at a time through the component's reactive props), so the UI core suites (`uiCore`, `uiLayoutWave1`, `uiTransforms`, `uiFilters`, `uiPaint`, `uiForms`, `uiScrolling`, `uiEvents`, `uiInteractions`, `uiNavigation`, `uiInlineRuns`, `uiAnimation`, `uiAnimationPerf`, `uiLocalisation`, `babylonUI`, `elementKinds`, `uiFeature`, `uiControlMapper`, `ui`) run through the renderer and host; tests of removed features (bindings, `list()`, `t:`, `<Use>`, documents' problems and hot reload, `uiDocument.test.ts`, `uiDocuments.test.ts`, `uiDocumentEdits.test.ts`, `uiEdit/textEdits.test.ts`, `uiLocalise.test.ts`) are deleted; `editor/uiHandles.test.ts` runs on a view's component; `uiPicking.test.ts`, `uiAnimationSources.test.ts`, `inspectorTarget.test.ts` re-pointed; new: nav references following ids (`uiNavigation.test.ts`), retired-document loads (`ui.test.ts`), gathering from Svelte (`gather.test.ts`). Semantics the host has that documents didn't, now in the tests: a number field's arrow-key steps are `input` events (its `change` comes on commit), and siblings settle their structural styles in the late sync rather than at build. **Sizes** (Vite build, gzip -9 of every js/css/wasm file): player 7,570,761 → 7,518,475 bytes (−52.3 KB; the 39.2 KB `parsers` chunk is gone, `myco` −7.8 KB; the cascade and selector reader now load as one `hostSupport` chunk, 13.9 KB); editor 27,084,903 → 26,995,631 (−89.3 KB, its player copy included; the Localisation panel's chunk takes `htmlparser2` for XLIFF, +21.9 KB). e2e specs rewritten for views: `ui-navigation`, `localisation`, `arena` (its scoreboard read). **Wave F (2026-11-26): what wave D removed without a Svelte replacement, rebuilt.** `UIView.firstSelected` (a string field: the id of an element the component renders, `"options"` or `"#options"`) is written on the view's canvas as its `first-selected` reference, so the host resolves it as it resolves `nav-*` and it follows the id across re-renders (none once the element is gone or the field is empty); `uiNavigation.test.ts` covers it, and the `ui-navigation` e2e pause test starts on Options again. The localisation tools that read markup (unkeyed-text list with Dismiss, the text fit check, the Inspector's Localise / Extract to string table) work on `.svelte` sources: see 0356's row. |
| [0424](../decisions/0424-tweening-is-a-shared-rust-crate-linked-by-the-animation-core-and-a-small-tween-module.md) Tweening in a shared Rust crate, a standalone tween module | Partial | **Built:** `native/common/tween` (`myco-tween`, no_std + alloc: the tween table, `Spec`, the easings, the sequence program `seq`, the `tween` wire family, `CurveStore`; the curve lookup `Curves` is the one generic host parameter, no trait objects per frame, no memory type parameter) and `native/common/wire` (`myco-wire`, the record mechanism), both linked by the animation core (0370 row; core rebuilt: `myco_anim_bg.wasm` 868,216 B, budget benches unchanged). `myco-curve` has the key parser behind an `alloc` feature (off by default; the audio core links it without it, and its source hash now covers only `native/common/curve`). **The standalone tween module** (`native/tween`, `npm run build:tween` into the committed `src/engine/tween/wasm/`): the tween calls, a curve store and a **sequence player** (`native/tween/src/seq.rs`: the core's timeline playback cut down to sequences, the same `timeline` records and exports); 45,584 B raw, 20,014 gzip, size gate 53 KB (the spike's 36 KB predates the sequence player); a source hash over the crates it compiles (`build.rs`, `hash.json`, `src/engine/tween/wasm/sourceHash.test.ts`); `native/tween/tests/parity.rs` runs the same tweens, sequences and controls on the core and the module and compares every output bit for bit. `gen_wire` also writes `src/engine/tween/wasm/wire/{core,tween,sequence}.ts`. **The TypeScript backend** (`src/engine/tween/backend.ts`, internal): `TweenBackend` (create/control/update a tween table and its sequences, read rows, values, weights and change records), `openTweenBackend` (the provider at `TWEEN_BACKEND_PROVIDER`, else the standalone module loaded on demand, `standalone.ts`); the animation plugin contributes one over its core's table while active (`runtime/core/tweenBackend.ts`, its own World id in the core); `features/tweens.ts` unchanged. The record codec and `simd128` probe moved to `src/engine/wasm/` (`wireRecords.ts`, `simd.ts`). Tests: `standalone.test.ts`, the plugin's `tweenBackend.node.test.ts` (both backends, same scripts, bit-identical). UI animation (0422 wave 4) runs on it: `ui/animation/UIAnimator.ts`, tweens only (keyframe segments created as they come due), curves cached per CSS timing function. Svelte transitions (0423 wave Y2) run on it through `UIAnimator.play`, tweens only. |
| [0425](../decisions/0425-games-bundle-the-svelte-runtime-and-ui-renderer-into-their-own-code-at-export.md) Games bundle the Svelte runtime and UI renderer at export | Done | **Mounting:** a compiled component's module exports `$mount(host, props)` (appended by `plugins/ui/svelte/compile.mjs`: the renderer's `mountView` with that module's own component, so the Svelte and renderer it was linked with); `UIView` calls it and imports no Svelte (it loads only the cascade, on demand). Same in the editor (shared runtime from 0423, Play and hot reload unchanged) and in a game build. **Export:** the build target (`projectCode/buildTarget.ts`, now `buildTarget({ ui })`) keeps UI modules (components, rune modules, imported `.css` modules) aside and, when the link closes, bundles them (`projectCode/uiBundle.ts`, esbuild, minified, code splitting) with the Svelte runtime and the renderer: each module an entry at its own `code/` address (scripts that import a rune module get the bundle's instance), shared code in `code/ui-runtime/`; one Svelte instance per game. The runtime is resolved from the engine's files like npm packages (`PackageResolver`, which gained a package's `imports` map (`#…`) and extra conditions; `production` here), `esm-env`'s flags are defined (production, browser) so Svelte's dev branches fold away, the renderer's TypeScript is transpiled with the engine's emit settings. Sources: `npm run export` reads the engine checkout (`nodeUIRuntimeSource`, `server/packages.ts`); the editor's exports read the same files from `virtual:myco-ui-runtime` (`uiRuntimeFiles()` in `vite.shared.ts`, the closure `collectUIRuntimeFiles` reads: 128 files, a lazy 199 KB gzip chunk loaded on the first export with Svelte UI). `planWebBuild` takes `uiRuntime`; without it a project with Svelte UI is refused. **Player:** `uiRuntimeEntries()` and the fixed-name Svelte entries are removed; `dist/player/` has no Svelte. The renderer's imports from the engine's own modules (`src/engine/`) resolve to the player's `myco-ui-host.js` (`uiHostEntry()` in `vite.shared.ts`, re-exporting exactly the value names the renderer imports, read from its source: today `Color`, `Vector2`, `uiTagOfHtml`, `setUIHostStyles`, `UIHostEvent`; 0.3 KB gzip), sharing myco.js's engine instance. **Sizes** (the export test's small HUD: two components, a rune module, an imported `.css`, a button; gzip per file, summed): its UI bundle 24.9 KB = Svelte runtime 18.6 KB + renderer 4.3 KB + components, rule data and chunk glue 2.0 KB; plus the cascade and selector reader the player loads on demand, 10.7 KB in this build (`hostSupport` 1.5 + `cascade` 6.7 + `paintValues` 2.6; 8.7 KB at 0423 wave A part 2, before 0422 wave 3); about 35.9 KB of UI code in all against the 58.9 KB baseline and the ~32 KB estimate (the runtime matches the 18.5 KB estimate; the cascade grew). Tripwire: `export/svelteExport.test.ts` fails above 24.9 KB + 20%. Tests: `export/svelteExport.test.ts` (bundled code imports only build files, no `svelte/compiler`, no dev code; a keyed `{#each}` brings the each block and the HUD without one doesn't; the editor's served files bundle byte-identically to the checkout's; refused without a runtime; the tripwire; mounts in the player's layout through the real `myco-ui-host.js` entry code: props reactive, clicks reach handlers, a script shares the rune module's state), `editor/packages.test.ts` (subpath imports, conditions). **Not yet:** marking Svelte's modules side-effect free (it doesn't declare `sideEffects`; measured −1.6 KB, unverified as safe); the dedicated server links no UI code (0425's open question). |
| [0426](../decisions/0426-ui-events-propagate-through-capture-target-and-bubble-phases.md) UI events propagate through capture, target and bubble phases | Done | **Records:** the UI core's routing (`ui/UICore.ts`) records the frame's events with their default actions (`ui/events.ts`: `UIEventRecord`, `UIEventBase`, `propagate`, listener lists with `capture`/`once`/`passive`, `dispatchUIEvents`; DOM key names `uiDomKey`/`uiDomCode`); the interaction log (`ui/interactions.ts`) publishes them with its frame rule, beside the polled events (each polled event also records itself; focus from a press is published as it's dispatched, cause `press`). The device snapshot hands routers the pointer type, the wheel and the frame's keys (`PointerFrame`, internal). **Event set:** `pointerover`/`pointerout`/`pointerenter`/`pointerleave`, `pointermove`, `pointerdown`/`pointerup` (every button), `click` on the element press and release share (none in a non-interactable button), `dblclick`, `contextmenu`, `wheel`, `pointercancel` (a touch scroll starting), `keydown`/`keyup` (the focused element, else the scroller last pressed in), `focusin`/`focusout` after `focus`/`blur`, UI drags from the new `draggable` field (`dragstart`, `drag`, `dragenter`, `dragover`, `dragleave`, `drop`, `dragend`, `UIDataTransfer`), `scroll`/`scrollend` (0427). Targets are raycast targets (0385); engine-made labels pass their events to their parent. **Dispatch:** `engine.UIDocumentEvents` dispatches every record, documents' and hosted trees', in order, after filling `UIEvents` as before; `engine.UIHostEvents` keeps transition and animation events. **Default actions:** focus on press (`pointerdown`), scrolling (wheel, touch drag, keys: 0427), a drag starting (`dragstart`), submit on Enter (`keydown`; prevented, the polled `submit` is retracted). **Front ends:** `UIDocumentElement.on(type, handler, { capture, once })`/`off`, the `UIEvent` interface's DOM members and fields; `UIHostElement.on(type, fn, { capture, once })`, `dispatch` through ancestors, `UIHostEvent extends UIEventBase` with `relatedTarget`, `onUIHostBubbleOut`; the renderer (`plugins/ui/svelte/renderer.ts`) relays each type per phase, `addEventListener` with `capture`/`once`/`passive`, the new `on…` names (and `mouse…` aliases), `dispatchEvent` through the nodes and out to the globals, `draggable` as a field; `<svelte:body>`/`<svelte:document>`/`<svelte:window>` continue what bubbles out of a view and skip the devices' feed of types the UI dispatched that frame (`globalTargets.ts` `_continue`). Tests: `engine/tests/uiEvents.test.ts` (phases, stopping, once/off, pointer data, enter/leave, contextmenu, disabled clicks, focus default and its prevention, keys, prevented submit, drags), `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewEvents.test.ts` (Svelte capture/bubble to the window, stopPropagation, keys to the window once, `dispatchEvent` with capture/passive/once). Missing: capture from the window/document down (global capture listeners hear UI events only on the way up), touch events (`touchstart`…). Checkbox toggling and the other form defaults: 0428 (its row). **`inert` (conformance engine round):** `UIElement.inert` (documents: `inert="true"`; Svelte: the attribute, which outros write) takes the element and its subtree out of hit-testing (the pointer reaches what is behind, so no event targets them), focus (a script's `focused = true` does nothing; focus inside leaves) and navigation; `isInertUI`. The Element subset's `focus()`/`blur()` dispatch `blur`/`focusout`/`focus`/`focusin` at once. Tests: `engine/tests/uiEvents.test.ts`, `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewEvents.test.ts`. |
| [0427](../decisions/0427-ui-elements-scroll-with-overflow-auto-and-scroll.md) UI elements scroll with overflow auto and scroll | Done | **Fields** (`ui/components.ts`): `overflow` gains `auto`/`scroll`; `overflowX`/`overflowY`, `scrollX`/`scrollY`, `scrollBehavior`, `overscrollBehavior`, `scrollbarWidth`, `scrollbarThumbColor`/`scrollbarTrackColor`, `draggable` (0426); shorthands `overflow: x y` and `scrollbar-color` (`ui/document/cascade.ts`); axes resolved by `overflowAxes` (`ui/elementKinds.ts`, CSS's visible-beside rule), and a panel the player scrolls is a raycast target. **Core** (`ui/UICore.ts`): scroll state per clipping element (`UILaidOutElement.scroll`: offsets, ranges, client box, scrollbars; `reach` for navigation), extent from children's margin boxes plus end padding, clamped offsets written back by the routing core, children laid out with a default shrink of 0 along a scrolling main axis, offset drawing, hit-testing (scrollbars first), picking and `cornersOf` through the offsets; input as default actions: the wheel with per-axis chaining and `overscroll-behavior` (claims the game's wheel, `InputClaim.wheel`), touch/pen drag scrolling past 10 px with `pointercancel` and momentum, keys (Page/Home/End from the focused element's scroller; arrows with nothing focused), scrollbar thumb drags and track paging; `scroll`/`scrollend` recorded at layout. **Scrolling module** (`ui/scrolling.ts`): `scrollTo`/`scrollBy`/`scrollIntoView` (`block`/`inline`), smooth scrolling (CSS `ease`, 0.2–0.5 s, in the core rather than on the tween backend: see the record's as-built notes), momentum, the DOM members' argument forms and sizes; on `UIDocumentElement`, `UIHostElement` and renderer nodes (`scrollTop`/`scrollLeft`, `scrollWidth`/`scrollHeight`/`clientWidth`/`clientHeight`, `scrollTo`, `scrollBy`, `scrollIntoView`). **Navigation** (`ui/navigation.ts`) searches `reach` and scrolls what it focuses into view. **Drawing** (`render/babylon/ui.ts`): scrollers clip as `hidden` does; scrollbars are a track and a thumb per axis, drawn with the scroller's transform over its content. Tests: `engine/tests/uiScrolling.test.ts` (layout, offsets, clamping, hit-testing, `cornersOf`, unconstrained text, axes and shorthands, wheel with prevention and the game's wheel, chaining and `overscroll-behavior`, touch drag with cancel and momentum, no mouse drag-scroll, keys, navigation, scrollbars and their styling, thumb drag and track paging, the script API, smooth scrolling, `scroll`/`scrollend`), `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewEvents.test.ts` (node members, `onscroll`, the wheel in a view). Missing: max-content text in horizontal scrollers, element scroll bindings (scroll snapping, the gutter and `*-reverse`/right-to-left scrolling: 0432 G4). |
| [0428](../decisions/0428-ui-gains-html-form-elements-built-from-ui-core-primitives.md) UI gains HTML's form elements, built from UI core primitives and styled by the cascade | Done (part pseudo-elements left to a later record, as decided) | **Kinds** (`ui/components.ts`, `ui/elementKinds.ts`, markup tags): `UIToggle` (`Toggle`: `checked`, `indeterminate`, `name`, `value`, `accentColor`), `UIRadio` (`Radio`), `UISlider` (`Slider`: `min`/`max`/`step`/`value`), `UITextArea` (`TextArea`, an input field: `rows`, `wrap`, `overflow: auto`), `UISelect`/`UIOption` (`Select`/`Option`: `value`, `multiple`, `open`; `value`, `selected`, `label`), `UIProgress` (`Progress`, `value` null is indeterminate), `UIMeter` (`Meter`: `low`/`high`/`optimum`), `UILabel` (`Label`, `for`), `UIForm` (`Form`, `requestSubmit()`/`reset()`/`submitted`); `UIInputField` gains `type` (`text`/`password`/`number`) and `min`/`max`/`step`; `UIButton` gains `type` (`submit`/`reset`/`button`); `interactable` moves to every focusable control (`isFocusableUI`), `focused` and `changed` to their base. Public from `myco`. **Behaviour** (`ui/formControls.ts`): activation by click (the `click` record's default action), `submit` (navigation's click record, now cancelable with a default action) and labels (a click sent to the control, or focus for fields, sliders and selects); `input` then `change` records plus polled `changed`; radio groups per name and form (else canvas); selects keep `value` and options' `selected` in step (HTML's selectedness: the first enabled option without a choice); reset to the values the UI core first saw (`rememberDefaults`); `fireUIEvent` (`ui/events.ts`) dispatches a default action's or script's events at once once the frame's dispatch has started or passed, else with the frame's records. **Core** (`ui/UICore.ts`): a select's options lay out in a Yoga root of their own (its node is measured for its widest label), placed below it (above where it doesn't fit), outside clips, drawn and hit after everything else in its canvas, shown while `open`; a press outside closes it; slider press-jump and drag (`input` per move, `change` on release), Page Up/Down and Home/End; a typing number field's arrows step it; Enter in a one-line field submits its form after the field's own `submit`; a text area types into a hidden `<textarea>` (Enter doesn't submit), is `rows` lines tall and scrolls its wrapped text (0427); a password is measured and drawn as dots; every control takes focus on press (options leave it on their select). **Navigation** (`ui/navigation.ts`): left/right step a slider and move a named radio group's check; an open select's list takes up/down, Tab, Enter (choose) and Escape (close); opening from the keyboard focuses the chosen option. **Pseudo-classes** `:checked`, `:indeterminate`, `:placeholder-shown` (`UIPseudoState`: the new three optional) beside `:disabled`. **Parts** (`ui/formParts.ts`, `UILaidOutElement.parts`): rectangles in the element's frame (rotated for strokes) for a checkbox's fill and mark, a radio's dot, a slider's track/fill/thumb, a bar's or meter's fill (an indeterminate bar's sweeping segment on the unscaled clock, not the tween backend), a select's arrow, a chosen option's highlight; faded when not interactable. The default look is each component's constructor values (a stylesheet's rules all beat them), not a rule sheet: see the record's as-built note. **Drawing** (`render/babylon/ui.ts`): parts as Rectangles over the box; a select and a text area take the input field's text path (a text area's lines from the top, wrapped, scrolled; its caret by line breaks); navigation's focus colour on every control. **Documents** (`ui/UIDocument.ts`, `ui/document/markup.ts`): the tags, an option's and a label's text as a label child (as a button's), a text area's as its value; `input`/`reset` event types; the `UIEvents` buffer has controls' `focus`/`blur`/`change` and forms' `submit`. **Svelte** (`ui/host/htmlTags.ts`, `plugins/ui/svelte/renderer.ts`, `ui/host/UIHost.ts`): `input` by `type` (`uiTagOfInput`; unknown types warn and are text fields), `textarea`, `select`, `option`, `optgroup` (a panel), `progress`, `meter`, `label` (was text), `form` (was a panel); `type`, `name`, `for`, `wrap`, `maxlength`, `disabled` mapped; `checked`/`indeterminate`/`value`/`selected` content attributes (converted by the field's type) so the player's changes stay; an input whose `type` changes is remade in place; nodes' `checked`, `indeterminate`, `selected`, `options`, `selectedIndex`, `valueAsNumber`, `form`, `requestSubmit`, `submit`, `reset`, and `value` for every control (`uiValueOf`/`setUIValue`); the `bind` hook gives `bind:checked`, `bind:indeterminate`, `bind:group` (radios: the value; checkboxes: an array) and `bind:value` (number coercion on `number`/`range`; selects bind the option's value as given, any type, or an array with `multiple`; text areas), its listeners in the capture phase so bound state is set before the element's own handlers, re-read a microtask after the form's `reset`; no patch change. Editor: the UI document Create menu lists the new tags. Tests: `engine/tests/uiForms.test.ts` (checkboxes, radios and arrows, Space, prevented clicks, disabled, pseudo-classes and parts, labels, slider drag and keys, password, number arrows, text area layout/scroll/Enter, select pointer and keyboard, written values, progress and meters, form submit/Enter/reset/prevented reset, `:placeholder-shown`), `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewForms.test.ts` (tags and kinds, pushed bound values, label/radio/checkbox group bindings, slider/number/select bindings with numbers, text area binding, form submit and reset resync, `type` changes), `engine/tests/babylonUI.test.ts` (parts, a select's label). Missing: part pseudo-elements (`::-webkit-slider-thumb`, `::checkmark`; a later record), `<datalist>`, `<optgroup label>`, `<input type="date|color|file|submit|…">`, number spinners, `select size`, a text area's caret on soft-wrapped lines and following the caret when typing scrolls, keeping a select's `value` "" for a value no option has (the first option is chosen), the Svelte conformance re-run (the coordinator's). **Conformance triage (2026-11-26):** a checkbox or radio now toggles before its `click` handlers, as HTML's legacy pre-activation (the click record's `before`), and back if one prevents the click, else `input` and `change` follow (`uiClickRecord` in `formControls.ts`, used by routed clicks, navigation's submit and a label's click; `engine/tests/uiForms.test.ts`); `UIHostElement.click()` (`clickUI`) dispatches a script's click at once with that activation, its `input`/`change`/`submit` at once too (`dispatchUIEventNow`). |
| [0429](../decisions/0429-text-holds-styled-inline-runs-from-inline-html-and-contenteditable.md) A text element holds styled inline runs, which `{@html}` and `contenteditable` read and write | Partial (built; drawing captured in a real browser by `tests/e2e/ui-css.spec.ts`; a real-browser check of the IME and the caret missing) | **Runs** (`ui/elementKinds.ts` `textRunOf`, `ui/uiCoreTypes.ts` `UIInlineRun`, `UITextRun.runs`/`editable`): a text's inline children (texts and images, nested) resolved into styled runs (font, style, colour, decorations, background, `vertical-align`/shift, image, owner and source), whitespace collapsed across runs; plain text keeps 0271's single run; `sameTextMeasure` lets colour-only changes redraw without measuring. **Fields** (`UIText`): `fontStyle` (inherited), `textDecoration`, `backgroundColor` (a text's box; a run's glyphs), `verticalAlign`, `contentEditable`; `font-family` → `font`, generic families (`fonts.ts`). **Cascade** (`document/cascade.ts`): HTML's phrasing defaults (`TAG_DEFAULTS`, user-agent place), `#text` pieces match no rule. **Layout** (`ui/text/inlineLayout.ts`, pure): line breaking across runs with `lineSegments`, line boxes with a strut, baseline alignment and `vertical-align`, alignment, right-to-left run order, caret/hit/selection positions; tests `ui/text/inlineLayout.test.ts`. **Core** (`UICore.ts`): runs are entries without Yoga nodes (`inlineIn`; events go up through them, `up()`), measured and laid out by `layoutInline` (host `measureRunWidth`), published as `UILaidOutElement.inline`, run boxes from fragments, hits resolved to the run (`runAt`, `pickAll`); **`contenteditable`**: press/drag/Shift caret and selection, the hidden `<textarea>`, edits written into the runs' components (`ui/text/inlineEdit.ts`), `input`/`focus`/`blur` records, `UILaidOutElement.editing` (caret, selection). **Drawing** (`render/babylon/ui.ts`): `LocalisedTextBlock` draws the core's fragments (fonts, colours, backgrounds, decorations, inline images, outline, shadow, selection, caret) under the element's filters and transforms; `measureRunWidth`. **Svelte** (`plugins/ui/svelte/renderer.ts`): elements inside a text are runs (`br` a line break, `img` an inline image), text nodes beside them text pieces; `{@html}` through the patch's `html` hook and `htmlInline.ts` (`myco/ui-html`, imported by `compileSvelteUI` only into components using `{@html}`/`bind:innerHTML`; wired in `projectCode/uiModules.ts`, `uiBundle.ts`, `runtimeModules.ts`, `vite.shared.ts`); writable `innerHTML`, `innerText`; `bind:textContent`/`innerText`/`innerHTML`; `onUIHostEdit` (`ui/host/UIHost.ts`) brings nodes up to date before `input`. **Documents** (`document/markup.ts`, `UIDocument.ts`): phrasing child markup inside a `Text` (`MarkupElement.inlineTag`); keyed messages show their text without tags. **Localisation:** `tHtml(key, args)` (`localisation/t.ts`, exported from "myco"), string arguments escaped. Tests: `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewRichText.test.ts` (runs, wrapping, sub/sup/br/code/mark/img, a link run's click target, `{@html}` runs and panels, dropped scripts and handlers, `contenteditable` with `bind:textContent` and `bind:innerHTML`), `engine/tests/uiInlineRuns.test.ts` (document runs, collapsing, the core's wrapping across runs, `tHtml`, Babylon drawing the fragments), `plugins/ui/svelte/htmlInline.test.ts`, `plugins/ui/svelte/sveltePatch.test.ts` (the hook). Not built: `:hover`/`:pressed` on runs, run padding/margins/borders, inline blocks, `white-space` modes, undo, a blinking caret. |
| [0430](../decisions/0430-ui-draws-css-filters-svg-video-and-full-matrix-transforms.md) UI draws CSS filters, SVG, video and full matrix transforms | Partial (built; filters, inline SVG and skew captured by `tests/e2e/ui-css.spec.ts`, frame time measured (0422 wave 1 row); a real-browser check of the offscreen filter fallback and media missing) | **Matrix transforms:** `UIElement.transformMatrix` (`ui/components.ts`, `matrix(a, b, c, d, e, f)` text, "" none); `ui/transform2d.ts` composes `T(origin)·T(translate)·R·S·M·T(−origin)` and adds `parseMatrixText`/`matrixText`, CSS's 2D `decomposeCss`/`recompose` and `interpolateAffine`; `document/transformValues.ts` `parseTransform` reads `skew()`, `skewX()`, `skewY()`, `matrix()` and holds a list that doesn't fold as the matrix after its leading translates (`%` kept there; a later `%` refused). Cascade: `transform` covers `transformMatrix`, which is normalised as set. Animation: `animatedValues.ts` kind `matrix` tweens one progress float and decodes the CSS interpolation. Drawing: `render/babylon/ui.ts` `Drawn` mixin overrides a control's `_transform` with the element's Matrix2D when its map skews (left/top at the laid-out box, Babylon's rotation and scales reset); `UIHost.computedStyle().transform` appends the matrix. **Filters:** thirteen `UIElement` fields (`filterBlur` … `filterDropShadowBlur`); `ui/filters.ts` (steps in a fixed order, CSS text, reach, lengths through a transform); `document/filterValues.ts` `parseFilter` (folds repeated multiplicative functions, hue angles and blurs; refuses repeated grayscale/sepia/invert, several drop shadows, `url()`); the cascade's `filter` typed shorthand and transition longhands; the UI core publishes each element's chain (own, then ancestors', through each filterer's transform) as `UILaidOutElement.filter`; `Drawn` sets `ctx.filter` in `_applyStates` where Canvas2D has it (feature-detected per context) and otherwise renders the control offscreen and runs `render/filterPixels.ts` (Filter Effects colour matrices, three box blurs on premultiplied colour, drop shadow); redraw regions, `_intersectsRect` and clips grow by the filter's reach. `computedStyle().filter` reads the fields, so Svelte's `blur` transition works. **Inline SVG:** `UISvg` (`engine.UISvg`, tag `Svg`, kind image; `markup`, `color`); the Svelte renderer keeps `<svg>` descendants as nodes and writes the subtree's XML to `markup` on any change (`svgXml`, `refreshSvg`; the root's class/id/style stay the cascade's; size from `width`/`height`, else 300 × 150); the drawing layer's `MediaImage` rasterises it as an SVG image at the box's texture size (`svgForRaster`: root size, viewBox kept or made, `color` for `currentColor`), re-rasterised only when text, colour or size change, the old picture kept until the new loads. `ui/svg/svgGeometry.ts`: path data, shapes as paths, arc length (Gauss–Legendre), point at length, flattening, fill (nonzero/evenodd) and outline distance, SVG `transform` lists, `viewBoxMap`. Nodes' `getTotalLength()`/`getPointAtLength()`; pointer events retargeted from the `<svg>` to the shape under them by geometry and `pointer-events` (`svgShapeAt`), capture/target/bubble through its groups; `draw` through `animateSvgNode` (keyframes sampled on the `<svg>`'s animator clock into the shape's style; `measure` gives shapes their SVG paint). UI documents build `<Svg markup>` but not inline SVG children. **Media:** `UIVideo` (`engine.UIVideo`, tag `Video`, an image: `src`, `autoplay`, `loop`, `muted`, `volume`, `playbackRate`; `media` get/set, `play`/`pause`/`currentTime`/`duration`/`paused`/`ended`, `on`/`off` media events) drawn from its media element's current frame each frame while playing (`MediaImage`); `UIAudio` (`engine.UIAudio`, tag `Audio`, `display: none`) through `AudioSourceMedia`, an engine `AudioSource` on its node found by its registered name (time kept on the element's clock; `duration` NaN; `playbackRate` is pitch). `ui/media.ts` `UIMediaPlayer` keeps fields on the source each frame (`engine.UIMedia`, late update after the UI syncs), autoplays once, forwards media events, follows `volumechange`/`ratechange` back into the fields, pauses when the element is disabled. Renderer: media API on `<video>`/`<audio>` nodes, `<source src>`, media event relays, and `bind:currentTime`/`paused`/`volume`/`muted`/`playbackRate` (two-way) and `duration`/`ended`/`readyState`/`seeking`/`videoWidth`/`videoHeight` through the `bind` hook. Exported from `myco`: `UISvg`, `UIVideo`, `UIAudio`, `UIMediaSource`, `UIMediaEvent`, `UIMediaEventType`. Tests: `uiTransforms.test.ts` (matrix values, hit, decomposition, transition), `uiFilters.test.ts`, `svgGeometry.test.ts`, `babylonUI.test.ts` (skew through `_transformMatrix`, `ctx.filter`, offscreen fallback, SVG rasterising, video frames), `uiViewTransitions.test.ts` (`blur`, `draw`), `uiViewMedia.test.ts` (SVG document, lengths, shape events, media API and bindings with a fake media element handed to `media`, audio). **Missing:** `backdrop-filter`, SVG filters, SMIL; stylesheet rules on shapes inside an `<svg>`; `<text>`/`<use>` hit-testing; filters drawn per drawn piece rather than on the flattened subtree; audio `duration`. |
| [0431](../decisions/0431-ui-preferences-are-a-player-setting-read-as-user-preference-media-features.md) UI preferences as user-preference media features | Built (wave Z3) | `UIPreferences` (`engine/ui/preferences.ts`, public from `myco`): `reducedMotion`, `colorScheme` (the player's choice, else the system's `matchMedia`, followed live; `null` follows the system again), `reset()`, `onChange`; persisted under `settings/myco.ui`, loaded by the UI plugin's World service (`myco.uiPreferences`) before a runtime World's first scene and forgotten when it ends. `engine/ui/document/mediaQuery.ts`: `prefers-reduced-motion` (`no-preference`/`reduce`) and `prefers-color-scheme` (`light`/`dark`) with boolean forms, matched with or without a canvas; UI documents and hosted trees restyle when the values change (`uiPreferencesVersion`). Svelte: the canvas `MediaQuery` answers them, and `svelte/motion`'s own `svelte/reactivity` import resolves to it (`vite.shared.ts` `svelteUI()`, `projectCode/uiBundle.ts`), so `prefersReducedMotion` follows. Tests: `plugins/ui/runtime/uiViewPreferences.test.ts` (system default followed live, override and `null`, `@media`, `prefersReducedMotion`, `MediaQuery`, persistence and loading, refusals). **Missing:** `prefers-contrast`, `forced-colors`, `prefers-reduced-transparency`; an editor preview of the preferences. |
| [0432](../decisions/0432-the-ui-closes-its-remaining-css-text-scroll-and-tooling-gaps.md) The UI closes its remaining CSS, text, scrolling and tooling gaps | Done | **G4, scrolling:** CSS Scroll Snap (`scroll-snap-type`/`-align`/`-stop`, `scroll-padding`, `scroll-margin`; fields and shorthands in `ui/components.ts`, `ui/document/cascade.ts`) chosen by `ui/scrollSnap.ts` and applied by `ui/scrolling.ts` to `scrollTo`/`scrollBy`, keys, the wheel (after it settles), touch flings and thumb releases, `scrollIntoView` and navigation, re-snapping at layout (`ui/UICore.ts` `snapOf`, `uiResnap`); `scrollbar-gutter: stable [both-edges]` (a Yoga border on the bar's side); `*-reverse` and the new `direction` field (`dir`) scroll from the end with negative offsets (`UIScrollInfo.minX`/`minY`), the vertical bar on the left in `rtl`. Tests: `engine/tests/uiScrollSnap.test.ts`. **Missing (G4):** text runs following `direction`, `wrap-reverse` origins, transformed snap areas. **G1:** `:has()` with relative selectors (`SimpleSelector.has`, read by `selectorQuery.ts` for rule data and queries; `cascade.ts` `matchHas`, `hasUse`/`couldAnchorHas`; `UIHost.ts` restyles only candidate anchors, alone, their subtree when what they pass on changed); `em`/`rem`/`vw`/`vh`/`vmin`/`vmax` in every length and `calc()` (`cssMath.ts` `resolveRelativeUnits`, `cascade.ts` `UNITS_KEY`; viewport = canvas UI pixels, reference resolution before layout; `:root` text properties now inherit); `transition-behavior: allow-discrete` and `display`'s discrete rule (`animationValues.ts`, `animatedValues.ts`); `linear()` as linear curve keys (`timingCurves.ts`); reversing shortening and inherited values passed to children per frame, panels' pass-on fields animated (`UIAnimator.ts`, `UIHost._animatedWrite`). Tests `uiHasAndUnits.test.ts`, `uiAnimation.test.ts`. G2–G5 (pseudo-elements, `backdrop-filter`, text, scrolling, tooling) not built. **G5 (tooling):** type checking inside `.svelte` (svelte2tsx 0.7.63, editor only: `editor/code/svelteCheck.ts`, `sourceMaps.ts`, `monaco.ts` markers and Play's check, `scriptCheck.ts`), plus hover, completion, signature help and go to definition in components' scripts and template expressions (`monaco.ts` providers on the HTML language, the caret mapped into the generated TypeScript by `ComponentTypeScript.generatedOffset` / `sourceMaps.ts` `generatedPosition`, completion only where `inComponentCode`; e2e in `code.spec.ts`); reproducible wasm for every native build but nav (`native/rustflags.mjs` remapping, `native/rustc-wrapper.mjs` crate identities; anim, tween, audio rebuilt; `engine/wasm/reproducibleWasm.test.ts`); audio's `wasm-opt` opt-in (`MYCO_WASM_OPT=1`). Missing: nav's wasm still embeds paths (frozen), input pins no toolchain. **Missing (G4):** `wrap-reverse` origins, transformed snap areas. **G3, text:** `white-space` (shorthand of the inherited `UIText.whiteSpaceCollapse`/`textWrapMode`, own section in `ui/document/cascade.ts`; `text-wrap`) on plain texts (`textRunOf`) and per run (`nowrap`, `keepSpaces` in `ui/text/inlineLayout.ts`), the renderer passing text as written; runs' `:hover`/`:active` (`:active` parsed as `:pressed`) from the core's pointer paths (`UIInteractions.isTextHovered`/`isTextPressed`); inline boxes (`UITextRun.boxes`, `UIInlineLine.boxes`: padding, borders, horizontal margins, sliced) laid out, hit and drawn (`paintInlineBox` in `render/babylon/ui.ts`), `UIText` borders; atomic inline boxes (`isInlineAtom`: `display: inline-block`/`inline-flex`, controls, SVG, media, panels in text) in Yoga trees of their own (`UICore.layoutAtoms`, `atomPlace`), runs with `atom` sizes and baselines, drawn and hit as themselves; a text's base direction from `direction` (own or inherited) else the language's. Tests: `ui/text/inlineLayout.test.ts`, `engine/tests/uiInlineText.test.ts`. **Missing (G3):** floats, block elements breaking a paragraph, percentage widths in inline blocks, `tab-size`. **G2:** pseudo-elements in rule data (`Selector.pseudoElement`, `StyleTarget.pseudoElement`; both readers; specificity as a tag; `cascade.ts` `matchSelector`, `pseudoElementsUsed`, `CONTENT_KEY`, `contentValue.ts`); `::before`/`::after` generated by `UIHost.ts` (`generate`: a text run inside text elements, `UIText._pseudo` ordered by `elementKinds.ts`; a panel with a label elsewhere; first/last node, outside `children`, synthetic for events); `::placeholder`/`::selection` into `placeholderColor`, `placeholderFontStyle`, `selectionColor`, `selectionBackgroundColor` (`pseudoFields`), drawn by `render/babylon/ui.ts` (input selections via `UICore.selectionOf`, editable text's); part pseudo-elements into `formParts.ts` `UIPartStyle`s (`setUIPartStyles`; `UIPart` border and shadow); `backdrop-filter` as `backdrop*` fields (`filters.ts` `backdropFilterFields`, `UILaidOutElement.backdrop`), drawn by the `Drawn` mixin from a copy of the texture under the box with redraw regions grown around backdrop elements (`growForBackdrops`). Tests `uiPseudoElements.test.ts`, `uiFilters.test.ts`, `babylonUI.test.ts`. **Missing (G2):** see the record's as-built left-out list (generated boxes aren't `display: inline`; `::placeholder` fonts; part transitions; no 3D scene under a backdrop). |

`inputControlInfo` for a control's components). **Editor** asset kind `actionSet` (`editor/actionSetKind.ts`, New Action Set in `actionSetCreate.ts`: a free `game.New` id, listed in `input.actionSets`, opened with its name selected); `inputContext` opens the dialog, its Inspector editor and Input Context panel removed. **Input dialog** `editor/ui/input/` (File › Input…): list of sets (code sets read-only, clashes on both entries) and contexts with +, F2 / context menu (Rename, Duplicate, Copy id, Delete); set view (`ActionSetView.tsx`: name, key, path, actions A to Z with type, consume, Mapped in, Map it in default, a type change converting mappings in every context, a rename carried into contexts, Networking with precision and upload estimate from `actionSetModel.ts`); context view (`ContextView.tsx`: name, Used by from scene and prefab files, mappings from `MappingRows.tsx`, the searchable action picker `ActionPicker.tsx` / `actionPickerModel.ts` with Create in a set and New set, unsuited-control warnings, scripts' defaults read-only in default); one history over every file (`editor/inputDialogHistory.ts`) with Undo, Redo, an undo tree pane and the editor's Ctrl+Z; Saving / Saved / Live footer; removals undoable from a toast; container queries. **Since:** modifiers as an ordered stack (`SortableList`, ⋯ menu) with Try a value through the engine's own modifiers (`traceInputModifiers` in the editor SDK, implicit stick dead zone and type coercion included); triggers as chips in Any of / All of / Blocked by groups; a note per mapping (`MappingSpec.note`, editor-only); collapsible mapping cards; making, naming and deleting files are history steps, and deletes are undone from a toast; new projects and projects opened without it get one (`editor/defaultInputContext.ts`). A modifier can be turned off (`off: true`, skipped as if absent; Turn off / on in the stack's ⋯ menu), and while playing the stack shows the mapping's live value through each modifier (`inputMappingValue`, `editor/ui/input/liveMappingValue.ts`; plain keys pressed in the dialog reach the game). **Not in this build** (the record's open questions): Convert to asset, per-file action-name types in the checker. **Tests** `actionSetList`, `sortableListLogic`, `jsonModule`, `assetOps`, `inputDialogFiles`, `pickerFolds`, linker (`*.actions.json` imports), `projectSettingsModel`, `actionSetModel`, `actionSetKind`, `inputDialogModel`, `inputDialogHistory`, `actionPickerModel`, `inputContextModel`, `inputContextKind`, `inputDebug`, `actionAssets`, story `InputDialog.stories.tsx`, e2e `tests/e2e/input.spec.ts` (New Input Context in the dialog; a new set listed and mapped in default), `projectsettings-categories.spec.ts`. |
| [0434](../decisions/0434-ui-layout-moves-from-yoga-to-taffy-compiled-to-webassembly.md) UI layout moves from Yoga to Taffy | Done | **Crate:** `native/layout/` (workspace: `layout-core`, Taffy 0.16.0 own node store behind `u32` handles, numeric setters, a measure callback, 22-float boxes; `layout-wasm`, the wasm-bindgen 0.2.130 boundary with a box buffer in module memory). `npm run build:layout` writes the committed `src/engine/ui/taffy/wasm/` (369,315 B, 124.8 KB gzip at first, 353,546 B after the calc() follow-up; size tripwire 440 KB in `build.mjs`, first build + 20%). **Adapter:** `src/engine/ui/taffy/layoutTree.ts` (`loadLayoutEngine`, `LayoutTree`, `LayoutNode` with CSS-keyword setters, `MeasureFunction`, `LayoutBox`). **UI core:** `ui/UICore.ts` lays out on the adapter (one `LayoutTree` per core; canvases, select lists and inline atoms are roots in it; resolved `direction` per node; text measured with CSS's shrink-to-fit width and a per-entry cache by width, cleared when the run changes); the stopgap's container minimum sizes are gone, its empty-text 0 × 0 rule stays; `yoga-layout` is removed. Behaviour changes, as in CSS: an unset min size is the automatic (content) minimum, so items no longer shrink below their content unless they clip (`uiScrolling.test.ts` updated: rows under `overflow: hidden` keep their height); wrapped text takes its whole room. **Tests:** `cargo test` in `native/layout/` (9, the Yoga fit-content repro among them); `src/engine/tests/taffyLayout.test.ts` (11, the repro again through the adapter); `src/engine/tests/taffyConformance.test.ts` (58 flex cases against Chromium's boxes in `layoutConformance/chromium.json`, recorded by `npm run conformance:layout`; all match within 1/32 px, Yoga matches 43); the UI suites (`src/engine/tests/ui*.test.ts`); `tests/e2e/ui-css.spec.ts`'s Pirates HUD test (`@slow`) checks the batteries and hull bar are on screen afloat. **Verified 2026-11-27:** full Vitest (13,060 passed), `ui-css.spec.ts` and `ui-navigation.spec.ts` (10 passed, captures unchanged), the `@slow` frame-time tests (2 passed; Pirates HUD UI 10.8 ms a frame against its 14 ms tripwire, bars on screen afloat). **Measured** (Node, HUD-shaped trees): first layout 1.32 ms vs Yoga 2.23 ms at 350 nodes, 10.5 vs 20.4 ms at 3,501; box reads 4-5x faster; one-text re-layout under 0.1 ms on both. **Baselines** (follow-up): measure functions return a first baseline (`Measured` in layout-core, `setMeasured(w, h, baseline)`), the UI core reports texts' first lines' baselines; 6 baseline conformance cases (53 in all, all match Chromium), `uiLayoutWave1.test.ts` "baseline alignment". `uiCore.test.ts`'s layout-call test spies on `LayoutNode`'s setters instead of reading private fields; `applyCanvas` sends only changes. **calc()** (follow-up): layout-core's own node store (`crates/layout-core/src/tree.rs`, Taffy's low-level traits, `calc` feature) resolves mixed lengths during layout (`set_calc`, an index table addressed by `CalcHandle::from_bits`, adapter `{ px, percent }`); the UI core's stale-size second pass is gone; wasm 353,546 B (118.7 KB gzip); 5 `calc()` conformance cases (58 in all, all match Chromium), `cargo test` (13), `uiLayoutWave1.test.ts` "calc() mixing % and px". Verified after the follow-ups: full Vitest (13,075 passed), `ui-css.spec.ts` and `ui-navigation.spec.ts` (10 passed, captures unchanged), the `@slow` frame-time tests (2 passed; Pirates HUD UI 9.6 ms a frame, control mapper 3.1 ms). **Not done:** `display: grid`/`block` in UI documents (ADR 0434's open questions; to be discussed). |
| [0435](../decisions/0435-why-it-played-names-the-trigger-and-the-game-code-that-asked.md) "Why it played" names the trigger and the game code that asked | Done | **Engine:** `core/runningScript.ts` (`runningScript()`, `@internal`), set and restored by `World.invoke` (every behaviour hook and message) and `Scheduler.runUser` (defined systems). **Audio:** `ProfileSink.eventStart(instance, asset, cause?)` with `ProfileCause` (`mix/profileSink.ts`); `playlists/cause.ts` builds it while a recorder runs; `AudioSource`, `AudioZone`, `Audio.line` and `Audio.say` name themselves and their node (`PlaylistOptions.trigger`, `triggerNode`, `@internal`). **Capture:** `eventStart` with six args (trigger, node, behaviour, system in the `parameters` table) within format 2; `readCaptureEvent` answers `cause` only when present. **Studio:** `InstanceDecisions.cause` (`profiler/decisionModel.ts`), the GAME step's name is the trigger and its line the source, the full list in its tooltip and screen-reader text (`whyModel.ts`, `WhyGraph.tsx`); story `Studio/Profiler/Details` `WhyItPlayedFromScript`. **Tests:** `engine/tests/runningScript.test.ts`, `capture/playCause.test.ts`, `capture/events.test.ts` (round trip, old two-arg record), `profiler/decisions.test.ts`. **Not done:** the script file (no class-to-file map in a shipped build); code after an `await` reads no hook. |
| [0372](../decisions/0372-animation-graph-and-scripting-api-on-the-ecs.md) Animation graph and scripting API on the ECS | Partial | **Amended by [0378](../decisions/0378-the-animation-builds-api-additions-and-differences-amend-0370-to-0376.md)** (the build's added names and its differences from this record's text). **T20t:** a binding table per (clip, Animator) with the bulk scatter of the highest-weight clip's columns into `@component` fields (sub-paths), data components, Transform, MeshRenderer `material.*` overrides, morph weights by index and activation; one missing-target warning per clip and node; rebinding on hierarchy change; notifies and notify states delivered through `World._sendMessage(nodes, NamedMessage, e)` (lifecycle names and base members refused) and the `AnimationEvents` buffer; audio keys through `Audio.play` when audio is active; a control-key dispatch point (handlers in T22t); tests `features/properties.node.test.ts`, `engine/tests/behaviourMessages.test.ts`. `set`, `request`, `travel` (promise), `jump`, slot `play` with cue or explicit sections and promises, `stop`, `link`, `layerWeight`, `state` (Unity transition semantics from the debug report), `compute`, `bone()` overrides (host-side, post-propagation, one frame), `animatorParams`, `AnimatorStateChanges`; crossfade masks on the implicit graph; tests `features/graph.node.test.ts` (18), `graph/maskWeights.test.ts`, `graph/parameters.test.ts`. **T21 (sprites and 2D bones):** image `flipbooks` → clip `image.png#Flip/<name>` (host-written raw clip on `SpriteRenderer.sprite`), slice `skin` (bones, mesh, weights) in `importSettings.ts`; library `engine/animation/sprites/` (islands shared with the Sprite Editor, outline → simplified polygon → constrained Delaunay mesh, auto weights, 2D skeleton → `myco.skel@1`); `Skeleton2D` as a skeleton source, skinned slices through `mycoSkin` (JS executor) and `QuadBatch.setTriangles`, several slices sharing one skeleton by bone name, claimed sprites skipped by the sprite feature; tests `sprites/sprites2d.test.ts`, `features/sprites2d.node.test.ts`. **T40t–T42t:** `Animator.ik` (code targets, `ikTargets` bindings, pole hints), grounder rays through `Physics.raycast` (physics loaded with `import()` when active), `Animator.warp`, rig LOD from `rigCullDistance`, automatic `RIG_RESET` on teleports; RETARGET nodes bound at run time (the map reference and `Skeleton.retargetSource` name the source skeleton or profile; bone maps from import or `guess_bone_map`); pose databases loaded from the baked `*.posedb.bin` beside their source with host-written trajectory parameters; tests `features/ik.node.test.ts`, `retarget.node.test.ts`, `plugins/animation/matching.node.test.ts`. **T33 (networking):** replicated parameters, states, slot plays and links as four tick-stamped JSON string fields (255 bytes each, entries left out rather than cut); interpolated ghosts apply changes at the interpolation tick (a state jumps after differing for 0.1 s); hybrid predicted root motion (`animation.PredictedRootMotion` samples the root curve per tick through `CharacterController.move`, re-simulated ticks reuse the first play, server-only plays adopted, refused plays stopped; the core's root delta not applied twice); gameplay-only servers (`rootMotionOnly` unless a BoneAttachment follows the skeleton); tests `features/network.node.test.ts` (8 two-World tests). Missing: linked-graph time sync, implicit-graph play replication, morph target names, a public IK `space` option, flip and size on skinned sprites, timeline event keys and `AnimationEvent.weight` (need a record), promise resolution on World teardown, `.animgraph.bin` loading (needs a table sidecar), scoped state events for linked graphs. **Phase R:** the whole graph runtime in the core (0370 row). **Phase B, T01:** `src/plugins/animation/` with a manifest optional on physics and audio; `Animator`, `Skeleton`, `SkinnedMeshRenderer`, `Skeleton2D`, `BoneAttachment`, `TimelineDirector` and the `animation.Tweens` data component with all fields; the buffers `AnimationEvents`, `AnimatorStateChanges`, `RootMotion`; built-in systems (`animation.PredictedRootMotion` and `FixedEvaluate` in fixedUpdate before the physics pushes; `Timeline`, `Gather`, `Evaluate`, `Apply` at the start of lateUpdate); the full public API declared in `myco` and stubbed with the building task named; feature, stage and registration seams; the `PHYSICAL_ANIMATION` ragdoll contribution point. Tests: `plugins/animation/plugin.test.ts`, `components.test.ts`, `editor/editorHalf.test.ts`. **T17:** `Skeleton` (asset acquired through the core host), the code-first `Animator` (`play`, `crossfade`, `stop`, `layerWeight`, `state`, `onFinished`, `defaultClip`, update modes normal/unscaled/fixed; promises resolve finished/interrupted/stopped and never hang), root motion in all four modes (the `RootMotion` buffer always filled; CharacterController and RigidBody only with physics), culling and LOD (CPU sphere-frustum against enabled Cameras), `BoneAttachment` through an internal `World._afterPropagation` hook, and the `skeletonInstances` render-sync contract; tests `features/playback.node.test.ts` (21), `assetInfo.test.ts`, `engine/tests/worldFrame.test.ts`. **Musical timing (T72):** `at: beat|bar|exit` and `align: start|hit` on `play`, `crossfade`, `travel`, `request`, held host-side and released in `animation.Gather` on the frame the heard audio clock crosses the boundary, with the late start applied to plays; hit alignment releases early by the hit's lead; `exit` follows reported Exit markers (`onMarker`); without audio, without `music`, or when the music stops: one warning and it happens at once. **Follow-ups (T70b):** `jump` with `normalizedTime` (graph wire v2; the network client re-enters a drifting ghost at the server's time), match debug through `AnimationCore.setMatchDebug` and a play-session `MatchDebugSource` feeding T55's viewport view, ragdoll generation with `Skeleton.profile`. Missing: a server flag to skip skinning (masks, slots, sections, graph instances and musical timing came later: T32, T72). |

one clip undo step per drag via undo-step gesture numbers), Inspector key buttons (`registerFieldAdornment`), preview in the Editor World through the runtime (each edited version under its own in-memory path at speed 0) on the scene being edited: Animators, Skeletons and TimelineDirectors activate in edit mode but stay inert until a preview drives them, and stopping puts the bones back to rest (D136); an `EvaluateTap` on the World core) whose scrub re-evaluates exact poses in a scratch instance and shows them while paused; the compile gains transition and condition path tables; editor SDK `selection.live()`, `playPaused`, `redrawPlay()`. The ragdoll builder: Generate from the skeleton (core description builder → `generateRagdollBones`), a validated bone table, shape/cone/twist gizmos with handle drags, Test Drop in a playing game (live `mode = "ragdoll"`, Reset restores); the animation runtime's public `authoring.ts` facade for the ragdoll editor; ~28 stories written. **T53 (Timeline window, `editor/timeline/`):** tracks grouped by bound node with binding pickers (scene nodes under the director, or the selected node), clips as bars on the shared `DopeSheetGrid` (move, trim, split, duplicate, ease handles), blend regions where clips overlap, animation/subclip/activation/audio/control/camera-cut/nested-timeline/property tracks, markers and signals, a breadcrumb into nested timelines (3 levels), scrub/play/loop through the TimelineDirector in the Editor World, asset drops onto rows; every edit one undo step; 10 stories written. **T18b (posed asset previews):** the live preview host runs a plugin-described World (`PosedPreviewSpec`, `ThumbnailRendererApi.previewPosed`; SDK `LargePreview posed` and `assetNodesFor`), live (non-edit-mode), stepped each frame and torn down with the preview; the clip preview, the clip import preview (the draft's cut clip before Apply) and the retarget side by side (source and retargeted bytes in one World) pose models at the playhead through the core. Lacks per-Animator rows and skinning ms (the core reports neither; needs a timing facility in the core). **T73 (takes):** plugins add take records through `engine/audio/take/extensions.ts` (types 0x40–0x7e) and `context.take.source`; animation writes `ANIM_FRAME` 0x40 plus `ANIM_TARGET` 0x41, `ANIM_KEY` 0x42, `ANIM_RAW` 0x43 (delta rows) and `ANIM_SELECT` 0x44 for up to 16 Animators; the graph editor opens a `*.sporesample` (`takeSource.ts`, read-only, always paused) and re-evaluates poses from a take keyframe bit for bit when the graph hash and bone count match, shown on an Animator of the same graph in a paused play session; compatibility notes when the project changed; 1 story file written. Lacks pose display in edit mode and cross-referencing audio marks with the animation clock. **E2E (T81):** every tool above has a Chromium spec (`tests/e2e/animation-*.spec.ts`), plus a capture-based runtime smoke and a cold-loaded export. Missing: dropping Hierarchy nodes onto a binding, editing the director's `bindingTracks`/`bindingNodes`, moving clips across tracks, mirrored clips and custom features in the bake, custom profiles in ragdoll generation, Test Drop starting play mode, onion-skin ghosts in the viewport (frames and a window overlay only), bone record mode, data-component tracks, the core's per-role guess scores, `.anim.json` output from bakes, right-click on bone rows, canvas groups/notes/copy-paste (need a format record), draggable rig handles, and no later tools. |
